Add a filter at the top of the scene panel: \\\"All scenes\\\" / \\\"Unassigned only\\\" toggle.\\n\\n**Step 6: Keyboard shortcut.**\\nAdd `L` key to toggle `assignMode` (add to existing keyboard handler). When in assign mode, `Escape` exits assign mode.\",\n   184→    \"priority\": 4,\n   185→    \"category\": \"panel-review\",\n   186→    \"status\": \"done\",\n   187→    \"created_at\": \"2026-03-16T16:00:00Z\",\n   188→    \"completed_at\": \"2026-03-17T16:46:50.677418+00:00\"\n   189→  },\n   190→  {\n   191→    \"id\": \"lovable-v9-05\",\n   192→    \"prompt\": \"Animatic preview: sequential panel + music playback as a timed slideshow.\\n\\n**Step 1: Add animatic data to project.**\\nIn `src/hooks/useProjectData.ts`, add to the `Scene` interface: `panelDuration?: number` (seconds per panel, default 4), `transitionType?: 'cut' | 'crossfade' | 'fade-black'` (default 'crossfade'). These are used by the animatic player. Include them in import/export normalization.\\n\\n**Step 2: Create AnimaticPlayer component.**\\nCreate `src/components/AnimaticPlayer.tsx`. Props: `scenes: Scene[]`, `mjGrids: MJGrid[]`, `onPlayMusic?: (src: string | null, title?: string) => void`, `assetBaseUrl: string`. The component is a fullscreen-capable player that shows panels sequentially with transitions and synced music.\\n\\n**Layout:** A dark container (`bg-black relative flex flex-col h-full`). Top: a thin toolbar (`h-10 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur flex items-center px-4 gap-3 z-10`). Center: the panel display area (`flex-1 flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden`). Bottom: a timeline scrubber bar (`h-12 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur px-4 flex items-center gap-3`).\\n\\n**Step 3: Panel sequencing logic.**\\nBuild a flat `animaticFrames` array via `useMemo`: iterate scenes, for each scene find grids where `grid.sceneId === scene.id` (or matching by index), take the selected quadrant URL (or gridImageUrl), creating one frame per assigned panel: `{ sceneIndex, panelIndex, imageUrl, duration, transition, musicUrl?, musicTitle? }`. Use `scene.panelDuration || 4` for duration. Store state: `currentFrame` (index), `isPlaying` (boolean), `elapsed` (seconds within current frame).\\n\\n**Step 4: Playback engine.**\\nUse `useRef` for a `requestAnimationFrame` loop. When `isPlaying`: increment `elapsed` by delta time each frame. When `elapsed >= currentFrame.duration`, advance to next frame (reset elapsed, apply transition). On frame change: if the new frame's scene has a different `musicUrl` than the previous, call `onPlayMusic(musicUrl, musicTitle)`. Keyboard: Space=play/pause, Left/Right=prev/next frame, Home=first frame.\\n\\n**Step 5: Panel display with transitions.**\\nRender current and previous frame images. Use CSS transitions for effects: `crossfade` = current image fades in over 0.5s (`opacity 0\\u21921`) while previous fades out, `fade-black` = previous fades to black then current fades in (1s total), `cut` = instant swap. Apply `object-contain w-full h-full` to images so they scale without cropping. Show a subtle scene label overlay in the bottom-left: `absolute bottom-4 left-4 text-white/60 text-sm font-mono bg-black/40 px-2 py-1 rounded`.\\n\\n**Step 6: Timeline scrubber.**\\nThe bottom bar shows: play/pause button (`Play`/`Pause` icons), current time / total time (`text-xs font-mono text-white/70`), a progress bar (`flex-1 h-1.5 bg-white/20 rounded-full cursor-pointer` with a filled portion `bg-primary rounded-full`). Clicking the bar seeks to that position. Below the bar, show tiny scene markers \\u2014 vertical ticks at each scene boundary, colored by whether the scene has assigned panels (green) or not (amber). Total time = sum of all frame durations.\\n\\n**Step 7: Wire into existing Animatic tab.**\\nThe app already has an 'animatic' ViewMode (line 20 of Index.tsx). In `TabContent.tsx`, replace the current animatic render block with `<AnimaticPlayer scenes={scenes} mjGrids={mjGrids} onPlayMusic={onSetMusicTrack} assetBaseUrl={settings.assetBaseUrl} />`. Pass `onSetMusicTrack` (the existing `setMusicTrack` API handler from Index.tsx) and `assetBaseUrl` from settings store. The MiniPlayer at the bottom of Index.tsx handles actual audio playback \\u2014 AnimaticPlayer just tells it what to play.\",\n   193→    \"priority\": 5,\n   194→    \"category\": \"music-integration\",\n   195→    \"status\": \"done\",\n   196→    \"created_at\": \"2026-03-16T16:00:00Z\",\n   197→    \"completed_at\": \"2026-03-17T16:47:10.846142+00:00\"\n   198→  }\n   199→]"}]},"uuid":"37df1ce4-4c1a-4918-9c6b-0457cd5c5ce3","timestamp":"2026-03-17T23:33:54.842Z"},"type":"agent_progress","prompt":"","agentId":"a8e4d65d7bcacba52"},"toolUseID":"agent_msg_01VH282Nhje2GKj6TGrKvvko","parentToolUseID":"toolu_01TkkGemouoauTPVMcp3DLWB","uuid":"dab0d64c-e3d6-4e1b-9d13-c33c89a07e42","timestamp":"2026-03-17T23:33:54.896Z","userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"00fff2bb-a8cc-4993-aadf-6662019d001b","version":"2.1.77","gitBranch":"main","slug":"buzzing-swinging-map"}

The current implementation loses scroll position when navigating with keys.\"),\n    (2, \"fix\", \"Persist compare selection across filter changes in GalleryViewer. When the user selects 2+ items for compare mode then changes a filter (status, episode, search), the selection is lost. Store selected item IDs in a ref so they survive re-renders from filter state changes.\"),\n    (3, \"fix\", \"Fix episode audio time sync: when playing an episode in the VideoViewer filmstrip, the MiniPlayer audio should sync its playback position to match the current clip's timestamp in the episode timeline. Currently audio and video play independently.\"),\n    (4, \"fix\", \"Add accessibility attributes to all Gallery interactive controls: aria-labels on icon-only buttons (zoom, compare, approve, reject), keyboard focus indicators matching the dark theme, alt text on all thumbnail images using the item's label field.\"),\n    (5, \"fix\", \"Read clip duration from actual video metadata in VideoViewer. Currently clip durations are hardcoded or missing. Use the HTML5 video element's 'loadedmetadata' event to read naturalDuration and store it in clip state.\"),\n    (6, \"fix\", \"Calculate and display episode total duration in the episode list. Sum all clip durations (from video metadata) for each episode. Show as 'MM:SS' badge on the episode card. Update dynamically as clips are added/removed.\"),\n    (7, \"fix\", \"Add scrubbing within a clip in VideoViewer: clicking on the progress bar or dragging the playhead should seek to that position in the current clip's video. Wire the click handler to video.currentTime.\"),\n    (8, \"fix\", \"Highlight the currently playing clip in the VideoViewer filmstrip. Add a visible border or glow effect (ring-2 ring-primary) to the filmstrip thumbnail that matches the actively playing video. Update highlight as playback advances through clips.\"),\n    (9, \"fix\", \"Add video playback rate selector to VideoViewer. Add a dropdown or button group (0.5x, 1x, 1.5x, 2x) that sets video.playbackRate. Persist the selected rate in localStorage so it carries across clips.\"),\n    (10, \"fix\", \"Auto-reload canvas data on poll: when polling is active and new data arrives via refetchInterval, merge the updated data into the canvas state without losing user edits (review decisions, scene assignments). Show a subtle toast 'Data refreshed' when new items arrive.\"),\n    (11, \"fix\", \"Add a pipeline status bar at the bottom of the app showing: total assets, pending count, generating count, done count, failed count. Use color-coded badges. Update in real-time from pipeline state. Clicking it navigates to the Pipeline tab.\"),\n    (12, \"fix\", \"Add new episodes notification: when polling detects new episodes not in the current state, show a toast notification with count and a 'View' button that navigates to the new episode. Don't auto-navigate — let the user decide.\"),\n    (13, \"fix\", \"Add a keyboard shortcuts help overlay. Trigger with '?' key. Show a modal listing all keyboard shortcuts organized by context (global, gallery, video, reader, review). Include the shortcut key combo and description for each.\"),\n    (14, \"fix\", \"Fix dark/light theme toggle visibility: ensure the theme toggle in SettingsDrawer actually switches the entire app between dark and light themes. Check that all components respect the 'dark' class on the root element and that text/background contrast meets WCAG AA.\"),\n    (15, \"fix\", \"Remember last-viewed episode and clip position in localStorage. On app load, restore the user to where they left off: same episode, same clip, same scroll position in the filmstrip. Add a 'Continue where you left off' banner if returning state is found.\"),\n]\n\nfor priority, category, prompt in bug_fixes:\n    queue.append({\n        \"prompt\": prompt,\n        \"status\": \"pending\",\n        \"priority\": priority,\n        \"category\": category,\n    })\n\nqueue_file.write_text(json.dumps(queue, indent=2))\nprint(f\"Added {len(bug_fixes)} bug fixes to queue\")\npending = sum(1 for i in queue if i[\"status\"] == \"pending\")\nprint(f\"Queue now: {len(queue)} total, {pending} pending\")\nPYEOF","description":"Add 15 bug fixes to lovable queue"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":969,"cache_read_input_tokens":52195,"output_tokens":1258,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":969,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CZ9XazGcwdcfFNsj1HMaR","type":"assistant","uuid":"d91b1f24-802d-4875-ae48-4248efd8cdd8","timestamp":"2026-03-17T23:35:12.158Z","userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"00fff2bb-a8cc-4993-aadf-6662019d001b","version":"2.1.77","gitBranch":"main","slug":"buzzing-swinging-map"}

/Users/eviemini/.claude/projects/-Users-eviemini-Documents-code-server-manager/00fff2bb-a8cc-4993-aadf-6662019d001b.jsonl:{"parentUuid":"3a6d0bb4-ad16-4a09-ba9f-69a7099b3077","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"2f248143-3266-4cc7-8b45-1e1ce9c26b87","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_017WaiG1tTpbWDgY1rbCX3RU","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→# Mardov Obsidian Vault — Canonical Lore Repository\n     2→\n     3→> **For Claude:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task.\n     4→\n     5→**Goal:** Build a git-committed, Obsidian-linked vault of Mardov campaign lore from all available sources — the single source of truth for all downstream creative work (webcomic, animation, music, wiki).\n     6→\n     7→**Architecture:** Obsidian vault at `mardov-vault/` in the server-manager repo. Every document uses `[[wikilinks]]` for cross-referencing. Source material stays on NFS (read-only reference); extracted/synthesized lore goes in git. Fleet agents extract, orchestrator reviews, human approves. Quartz 4 compatible for eventual web deployment.\n     8→\n     9→**Tech Stack:** Obsidian markdown with YAML frontmatter, `[[wikilinks]]`, git, fleet agents for parallel extraction, Whisper for audio transcription.\n    10→\n    11→---\n    12→\n    13→## Source Inventory\n    14→\n    15→| ID | Source | Location | Files | Extractable |\n    16→|----|--------|----------|-------|-------------|\n    17→| S1 | DM Chapter Notes | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/Chapters/` | 73 txt, 428 total | Text: direct copy. Images: link as assets. |\n    18→| S2 | Backstories | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/Backstories/` | 6 txt | Direct copy |\n    19→| S3 | NPC Reference | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/NPCs/` | 781 (697 img, 1 txt) | Image catalog, directory names = NPC names |\n    20→| S4 | PC Reference | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/PCs/` | 43 files | Image catalog, 23 character dirs |\n    21→| S5 | Places/Maps | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/Places/` | 1,220 files | Image catalog, directory structure = geography |\n    22→| S6 | Handouts | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/Handouts/` | 14 files | Direct copy |\n    23→| S7 | DM Notes/Ideas | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/Notes/` + `Ideas/` | 12 files | Direct copy |\n    24→| S8 | Magic Items | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/Magic Items/` | 3 entries | Direct copy |\n    25→| S9 | Inspiration Art | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/Inspiration/` | 74 files | Image catalog with filenames as descriptions |\n    26→| S10 | Recordings | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/Recordings/` | 20 files (2.5GB) | Whisper transcription needed |\n    27→| S11 | STL Minis | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/3D STL files/` | 12 files | Character list confirmation |\n    28→| S12 | Slideshow Images | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/Chapters/*/Slideshow/` | ~200+ | Visual scene reference per chapter |\n    29→| S13 | Visions | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/Visions/` | 2 files | Image reference |\n    30→| S14 | Stronghold Cards | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/Strongholds and Followers Cards/` | 21 files | Game mechanics reference |\n    31→| S15 | Character Sheets | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/Character Sheets/` | 9 files | Stats reference |\n    32→| S16 | DDB Characters | Needs re-scrape (CobaltSession cookie) | ~153 active | Full character JSON |\n    33→| S17 | Roll20 Campaign | Needs re-export (Chrome extension) | 4.25 GB | Chat logs, maps, journal, characters |\n    34→| S18 | Discord Art Chat | Needs Audrey's commission channel scrape | ~128 images | Character descriptions + commissioned art |\n    35→| S19 | Liz's Notes | User needs to provide | Unknown | Player-perspective source material |\n    36→| S20 | World Anvil | Blocked by Cloudflare | Unknown | World-building data |\n    37→| S21 | Game Recordings (Twitch) | User's Twitch (alfrado_sause) | 8 highlights | Short clips only, no full VODs |\n    38→\n    39→## Vault Structure\n    40→\n    41→```\n    42→mardov-vault/\n    43→├── MOC.md                          # Master index (Map of Content)\n    44→├── _templates/                     # Obsidian templates\n    45→│   ├── character.md\n    46→│   ├── location.md\n    47→│   ├── chapter.md\n    48→│   ├── faction.md\n    49→│   ├── item.md\n    50→│   └── creature.md\n    51→├── characters/\n    52→│   ├── pcs/                        # Player characters\n    53→│   │   ├── Daina Fireforge.md\n    54→│   │   ├── Sprocket Overspark.md\n    55→│   │   └── ...\n    56→│   ├── npcs/                       # Named NPCs\n    57→│   │   ├── major/                  # Recurring, plot-critical\n    58→│   │   ├── minor/                  # Named but not central\n    59→│   │   └── organizations/          # Factions, guilds, groups\n    60→│   └── creatures/                  # Monster types, beasts\n    61→├── locations/\n    62→│   ├── regions/                    # Mardov, Bynar, Monethern, etc\n    63→│   ├── cities/                     # DawnHaven, Argos, Vag'Mahar, etc\n    64→│   ├── dungeons/                   # Specific adventure sites\n    65→│   └── planes/                     # Outer Planes, Underdark, Moon\n    66→├── chapters/\n    67→│   ├── act-1/                      # Chapters 0-40\n    68→│   ├── act-2/                      # (part of Act 1&2 in source)\n    69→│   ├── act-3/                      # Chapters 41-86\n    70→│   └── act-4/                      # Chapters 87-101+\n    71→├── lore/\n    72→│   ├── cosmology.md                # Gods, Progenitors, Guard'Daemons\n    73→│   ├── magic-system.md             # Spell Plague, magic rules\n    74→│   ├── timeline.md                 # Master chronological timeline\n    75→│   ├── prophecies.md               # From DM notes\n    76→│   └── artifacts.md                # Magic items, legendary weapons\n    77→├── factions/\n    78→│   ├── Scarlet Tunic Brigade.md\n    79→│   ├── Vecna Sworn.md\n    80→│   ├── Therzadun Sworn.md\n    81→│   └── ...\n    82→├── production/\n    83→│   ├── visual-style-guide.md       # Locked dual-style system\n    84→│   ├── character-designs/          # Per-character visual specs\n    85→│   ├── glossary.md                 # D&D → Mardov term mapping\n    86→│   └── north-star.md              # Creative direction rules\n    87→├── sources/\n    88→│   ├── backstories/                # Raw text, linked from character pages\n    89→│   ├── handouts/                   # In-game documents\n    90→│   ├── dm-notes/                   # Raw DM planning notes\n    91→│   ├── transcriptions/             # Whisper output from recordings\n    92→│   └── ddb-export/                 # D&D Beyond character data\n    93→├── assets/                         # Binary files (images, audio)\n    94→│   ├── pc-art/                     # Commissioned + reference art per PC\n    95→│   ├── npc-art/                    # NPC reference images\n    96→│   ├── maps/                       # Location maps and images\n    97→│   ├── slideshow/                  # Chapter slideshow images\n    98→│   ├── inspiration/                # Reference art\n    99→│   ├── stl/                        # 3D mini files\n   100→│   ├── music/                      # Generated MusicGen tracks\n   101→│   └── panels/                     # MJ + ComfyUI panel images\n   102→└── .obsidian/                      # Obsidian config\n   103→    └── app.json\n   104→```\n   105→\n   106→## Frontmatter Templates\n   107→\n   108→### Character Template\n   109→```yaml\n   110→---\n   111→type: character\n   112→role: pc | npc-major | npc-minor | creature\n   113→name: \"Display Name\"\n   114→aliases: [\"other names\"]\n   115→race: \"\"\n   116→class: \"\"\n   117→affiliation: \"[[Faction Name]]\"\n   118→location: \"[[Location Name]]\"\n   119→status: alive | dead | unknown\n   120→first_appearance: \"[[Chapter X]]\"\n   121→source: [S2, S4, S16]  # Which sources contributed\n   122→art: [\"assets/pc-art/name.jpg\"]\n   123→---\n   124→```\n   125→\n   126→### Location Template\n   127→```yaml\n   128→---\n   129→type: location\n   130→category: region | city | dungeon | plane\n   131→name: \"Display Name\"\n   132→parent: \"[[Parent Region]]\"\n   133→notable_npcs: [\"[[NPC 1]]\", \"[[NPC 2]]\"]\n   134→chapters: [\"[[Chapter X]]\"]\n   135→source: [S5, S1]\n   136→maps: [\"assets/maps/name.jpg\"]\n   137→---\n   138→```\n   139→\n   140→### Chapter Template\n   141→```yaml\n   142→---\n   143→type: chapter\n   144→act: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4\n   145→number: 0\n   146→title: \"\"\n   147→pcs_present: [\"[[PC 1]]\"]\n   148→locations: [\"[[Location 1]]\"]\n   149→npcs_introduced: [\"[[NPC 1]]\"]\n   150→items_found: [\"[[Item 1]]\"]\n   151→source: [S1, S10]\n   152→has_recording: true | false\n   153→has_slideshow: true | false\n   154→---\n   155→```\n   156→\n   157→---\n   158→\n   159→## Phase 1: Vault Skeleton + Templates (Local, Task 1-3)\n   160→\n   161→### Task 1: Create vault structure and templates\n   162→\n   163→**Files:**\n   164→- Create: `mardov-vault/MOC.md`\n   165→- Create: `mardov-vault/_templates/character.md`\n   166→- Create: `mardov-vault/_templates/location.md`\n   167→- Create: `mardov-vault/_templates/chapter.md`\n   168→- Create: `mardov-vault/_templates/faction.md`\n   169→- Create: `mardov-vault/_templates/item.md`\n   170→- Create: `mardov-vault/.obsidian/app.json`\n   171→\n   172→**Step 1: Create all directories**\n   173→```bash\n   174→cd /Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager\n   175→mkdir -p mardov-vault/{_templates,characters/{pcs,npcs/{major,minor,organizations},creatures},locations/{regions,cities,dungeons,planes},chapters/{act-1,act-2,act-3,act-4},lore,factions,production/{character-designs},sources/{backstories,handouts,dm-notes,transcriptions,ddb-export},assets/{pc-art,npc-art,maps,slideshow,inspiration,stl,music,panels},.obsidian}\n   176→```\n   177→\n   178→**Step 2: Write MOC.md (Map of Content)**\n   179→Master index with links to all major sections. This is the vault's homepage.\n   180→\n   181→**Step 3: Write all 5 templates**\n   182→YAML frontmatter + section headers for each document type.\n   183→\n   184→**Step 4: Write Obsidian config**\n   185→```json\n   186→{\n   187→  \"useMarkdownLinks\": false,\n   188→  \"newLinkFormat\": \"shortest\",\n   189→  \"showLineNumber\": true\n   190→}\n   191→```\n   192→\n   193→**Step 5: Commit**\n   194→```bash\n   195→git add mardov-vault/\n   196→git commit -m \"feat: create Mardov Obsidian vault skeleton with templates\"\n   197→```\n   198→\n   199→### Task 2: Ingest backstories (direct text copy + wikilink enrichment)\n   200→\n   201→**Files:**\n   202→- Create: 6 character pages in `mardov-vault/characters/pcs/`\n   203→- Create: 6 source copies in `mardov-vault/sources/backstories/`\n   204→\n   205→**Step 1:** Copy raw backstory text files to `sources/backstories/` (preserving original).\n   206→\n   207→**Step 2:** For each backstory, create a character page using the template. Extract:\n   208→- Race, class, affiliation from text\n   209→- `[[wikilinks]]` to every named character, location, faction mentioned\n   210→- Link back to source: `Source: [[sources/backstories/Daina's Backstory]]`\n   211→\n   212→**Step 3: Commit**\n   213→```bash\n   214→git add mardov-vault/characters/pcs/ mardov-vault/sources/backstories/\n   215→git commit -m \"feat: add 6 PC backstories with cross-references\"\n   216→```\n   217→\n   218→### Task 3: Ingest handouts and DM notes\n   219→\n   220→**Files:**\n   221→- Create: 14 handout pages in `mardov-vault/sources/handouts/`\n   222→- Create: 12 notes in `mardov-vault/sources/dm-notes/`\n   223→- Create/update: lore pages for prophecies, magic system, zodiacs\n   224→\n   225→**Step 1:** Copy text files, add frontmatter, enrich with `[[wikilinks]]`.\n   226→\n   227→**Step 2:** Extract structured lore into dedicated pages:\n   228→- `lore/prophecies.md` from \"Prophesies and Zeniths.docx\" + \"Zodiacs and Player Symbolism.txt\"\n   229→- `lore/magic-system.md` from \"Magic Rules.txt\" + \"Spell Plague Notes 1-3\"\n   230→- `lore/artifacts.md` from \"Magic Items/\" contents\n   231→\n   232→**Step 3: Commit**\n   233→```bash\n   234→git commit -m \"feat: add handouts, DM notes, and extracted lore pages\"\n   235→```\n   236→\n   237→---\n   238→\n   239→## Phase 2: Chapter Extraction (Fleet — Parallel, Tasks 4-6)\n   240→\n   241→### Task 4: Extract Act 1&2 chapters (40 chapters)\n   242→\n   243→**Dispatch:** 2 fleet agents (worker-01, worker-02), 20 chapters each.\n   244→\n   245→**Per chapter, agent does:**\n   246→1. Mardov Obsidian Vault — Canonical Lore Repository\n\n> **For Claude:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task.\n\n**Goal:** Build a git-committed, Obsidian-linked vault of Mardov campaign lore from all available sources — the single source of truth for all downstream creative work (webcomic, animation, music, wiki).\n\n**Architecture:** Obsidian vault at `mardov-vault/` in the server-manager repo. Every document uses `[[wikilinks]]` for cross-referencing. Source material stays on NFS (read-only reference); extracted/synthesized lore goes in git. Fleet agents extract, orchestrator reviews, human approves. Quartz 4 compatible for eventual web deployment.\n\n**Tech Stack:** Obsidian markdown with YAML frontmatter, `[[wikilinks]]`, git, fleet agents for parallel extraction, Whisper for audio transcription.\n\n---\n\n## Source Inventory\n\n| ID | Source | Location | Files | Extractable |\n|----|--------|----------|-------|-------------|\n| S1 | DM Chapter Notes | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/Chapters/` | 73 txt, 428 total | Text: direct copy. Images: link as assets. |\n| S2 | Backstories | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/Backstories/` | 6 txt | Direct copy |\n| S3 | NPC Reference | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/NPCs/` | 781 (697 img, 1 txt) | Image catalog, directory names = NPC names |\n| S4 | PC Reference | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/PCs/` | 43 files | Image catalog, 23 character dirs |\n| S5 | Places/Maps | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/Places/` | 1,220 files | Image catalog, directory structure = geography |\n| S6 | Handouts | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/Handouts/` | 14 files | Direct copy |\n| S7 | DM Notes/Ideas | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/Notes/` + `Ideas/` | 12 files | Direct copy |\n| S8 | Magic Items | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/Magic Items/` | 3 entries | Direct copy |\n| S9 | Inspiration Art | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/Inspiration/` | 74 files | Image catalog with filenames as descriptions |\n| S10 | Recordings | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/Recordings/` | 20 files (2.5GB) | Whisper transcription needed |\n| S11 | STL Minis | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/3D STL files/` | 12 files | Character list confirmation |\n| S12 | Slideshow Images | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/Chapters/*/Slideshow/` | ~200+ | Visual scene reference per chapter |\n| S13 | Visions | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/Visions/` | 2 files | Image reference |\n| S14 | Stronghold Cards | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/Strongholds and Followers Cards/` | 21 files | Game mechanics reference |\n| S15 | Character Sheets | `synologyNFS/GoogleDrive/DnD/Mardov/Character Sheets/` | 9 files | Stats reference |\n| S16 | DDB Characters | Needs re-scrape (CobaltSession cookie) | ~153 active | Full character JSON |\n| S17 | Roll20 Campaign | Needs re-export (Chrome extension) | 4.25 GB | Chat logs, maps, journal, characters |\n| S18 | Discord Art Chat | Needs Audrey's commission channel scrape | ~128 images | Character descriptions + commissioned art |\n| S19 | Liz's Notes | User needs to provide | Unknown | Player-perspective source material |\n| S20 | World Anvil | Blocked by Cloudflare | Unknown | World-building data |\n| S21 | Game Recordings (Twitch) | User's Twitch (alfrado_sause) | 8 highlights | Short clips only, no full VODs |\n\n## Vault Structure\n\n```\nmardov-vault/\n├── MOC.md                          # Master index (Map of Content)\n├── _templates/                     # Obsidian templates\n│   ├── character.md\n│   ├── location.md\n│   ├── chapter.md\n│   ├── faction.md\n│   ├── item.md\n│   └── creature.md\n├── characters/\n│   ├── pcs/                        # Player characters\n│   │   ├── Daina Fireforge.md\n│   │   ├── Sprocket Overspark.md\n│   │   └── ...\n│   ├── npcs/                       # Named NPCs\n│   │   ├── major/                  # Recurring, plot-critical\n│   │   ├── minor/                  # Named but not central\n│   │   └── organizations/          # Factions, guilds, groups\n│   └── creatures/                  # Monster types, beasts\n├── locations/\n│   ├── regions/                    # Mardov, Bynar, Monethern, etc\n│   ├── cities/                     # DawnHaven, Argos, Vag'Mahar, etc\n│   ├── dungeons/                   # Specific adventure sites\n│   └── planes/                     # Outer Planes, Underdark, Moon\n├── chapters/\n│   ├── act-1/                      # Chapters 0-40\n│   ├── act-2/                      # (part of Act 1&2 in source)\n│   ├── act-3/                      # Chapters 41-86\n│   └── act-4/                      # Chapters 87-101+\n├── lore/\n│   ├── cosmology.md                # Gods, Progenitors, Guard'Daemons\n│   ├── magic-system.md             # Spell Plague, magic rules\n│   ├── timeline.md                 # Master chronological timeline\n│   ├── prophecies.md               # From DM notes\n│   └── artifacts.md                # Magic items, legendary weapons\n├── factions/\n│   ├── Scarlet Tunic Brigade.md\n│   ├── Vecna Sworn.md\n│   ├── Therzadun Sworn.md\n│   └── ...\n├── production/\n│   ├── visual-style-guide.md       # Locked dual-style system\n│   ├── character-designs/          # Per-character visual specs\n│   ├── glossary.md                 # D&D → Mardov term mapping\n│   └── north-star.md              # Creative direction rules\n├── sources/\n│   ├── backstories/                # Raw text, linked from character pages\n│   ├── handouts/                   # In-game documents\n│   ├── dm-notes/                   # Raw DM planning notes\n│   ├── transcriptions/             # Whisper output from recordings\n│   └── ddb-export/                 # D&D Beyond character data\n├── assets/                         # Binary files (images, audio)\n│   ├── pc-art/                     # Commissioned + reference art per PC\n│   ├── npc-art/                    # NPC reference images\n│   ├── maps/                       # Location maps and images\n│   ├── slideshow/                  # Chapter slideshow images\n│   ├── inspiration/                # Reference art\n│   ├── stl/                        # 3D mini files\n│   ├── music/                      # Generated MusicGen tracks\n│   └── panels/                     # MJ + ComfyUI panel images\n└── .obsidian/                      # Obsidian config\n    └── app.json\n```\n\n## Frontmatter Templates\n\n### Character Template\n```yaml\n---\ntype: character\nrole: pc | npc-major | npc-minor | creature\nname: \"Display Name\"\naliases: [\"other names\"]\nrace: \"\"\nclass: \"\"\naffiliation: \"[[Faction Name]]\"\nlocation: \"[[Location Name]]\"\nstatus: alive | dead | unknown\nfirst_appearance: \"[[Chapter X]]\"\nsource: [S2, S4, S16]  # Which sources contributed\nart: [\"assets/pc-art/name.jpg\"]\n---\n```\n\n### Location Template\n```yaml\n---\ntype: location\ncategory: region | city | dungeon | plane\nname: \"Display Name\"\nparent: \"[[Parent Region]]\"\nnotable_npcs: [\"[[NPC 1]]\", \"[[NPC 2]]\"]\nchapters: [\"[[Chapter X]]\"]\nsource: [S5, S1]\nmaps: [\"assets/maps/name.jpg\"]\n---\n```\n\n### Chapter Template\n```yaml\n---\ntype: chapter\nact: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4\nnumber: 0\ntitle: \"\"\npcs_present: [\"[[PC 1]]\"]\nlocations: [\"[[Location 1]]\"]\nnpcs_introduced: [\"[[NPC 1]]\"]\nitems_found: [\"[[Item 1]]\"]\nsource: [S1, S10]\nhas_recording: true | false\nhas_slideshow: true | false\n---\n```\n\n---\n\n## Phase 1: Vault Skeleton + Templates (Local, Task 1-3)\n\n### Task 1: Create vault structure and templates\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `mardov-vault/MOC.md`\n- Create: `mardov-vault/_templates/character.md`\n- Create: `mardov-vault/_templates/location.md`\n- Create: `mardov-vault/_templates/chapter.md`\n- Create: `mardov-vault/_templates/faction.md`\n- Create: `mardov-vault/_templates/item.md`\n- Create: `mardov-vault/.obsidian/app.json`\n\n**Step 1: Create all directories**\n```bash\ncd /Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager\nmkdir -p mardov-vault/{_templates,characters/{pcs,npcs/{major,minor,organizations},creatures},locations/{regions,cities,dungeons,planes},chapters/{act-1,act-2,act-3,act-4},lore,factions,production/{character-designs},sources/{backstories,handouts,dm-notes,transcriptions,ddb-export},assets/{pc-art,npc-art,maps,slideshow,inspiration,stl,music,panels},.obsidian}\n```\n\n**Step 2: Write MOC.md (Map of Content)**\nMaster index with links to all major sections. This is the vault's homepage.\n\n**Step 3: Write all 5 templates**\nYAML frontmatter + section headers for each document type.\n\n**Step 4: Write Obsidian config**\n```json\n{\n  \"useMarkdownLinks\": false,\n  \"newLinkFormat\": \"shortest\",\n  \"showLineNumber\": true\n}\n```\n\n**Step 5: Commit**\n```bash\ngit add mardov-vault/\ngit commit -m \"feat: create Mardov Obsidian vault skeleton with templates\"\n```\n\n### Task 2: Ingest backstories (direct text copy + wikilink enrichment)\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: 6 character pages in `mardov-vault/characters/pcs/`\n- Create: 6 source copies in `mardov-vault/sources/backstories/`\n\n**Step 1:** Copy raw backstory text files to `sources/backstories/` (preserving original).\n\n**Step 2:** For each backstory, create a character page using the template. Walk `Places/` directory tree → hierarchy IS the geography\n   - Top level: regions (Bynar, DawnHaven, Islands, Monethern, Vartos, etc)\n   - Sub-dirs: cities, dungeons, specific locations\n2. Create location pages with:\n   - Parent/child `[[wikilinks]]` (region → city → dungeon)\n   - Map images linked from `assets/maps/`\n   - NPCs associated (from NPC pages)\n   - Chapters where location appears\n3. Copy map images to `assets/maps/`\n\n**Commit after review.**\n\n### Task 9: Faction pages from NPC organizations + chapter context\n\n**Dispatch:** 1 fleet agent\n\nExtract from `NPCs/Organizations/`, `NPCs/Vecna Sworn/`, `NPCs/Therzadun Sworn/`, etc:\n- Faction pages with member lists (`[[wikilinks]]` to character pages)\n- Allegiances, goals, conflicts\n- Cross-reference to chapters and locations\n\n### Task 10: PC pages enrichment from all sources\n\n**Dispatch:** 1 fleet agent per PC (or 2 agents covering all 23)\n\nFor each PC directory:\n1. Gather: backstory, commissioned art, character sheet, DDB data (when available)\n2. Create comprehensive character page:\n   - Full backstory (linked to source)\n   - Art gallery (`![[assets/pc-art/...]]`)\n   - Chapter appearances (grep all chapter files)\n   - Relationships (`[[wikilinks]]` to other PCs, NPCs, factions)\n   - Key moments timeline\n   - STL mini reference (if exists)\n\n---\n\n## Phase 4: Audio Transcription (Fleet — GPU, Tasks 11-12)\n\n### Task 11: Whisper transcription of recordings\n\n**Dispatch:** GPU node (1080 Ti) or Tiny PC (3060 Ti)\n\n**20 recordings (2.5 GB):**\n- Chapter 62: 3 character files (Fen, Varys, Randall, Sprocket)\n- Chapter 63: 4 files (Brigit x2, Fen, Randall, Varys x2)\n- Chapter 64D: 3 files (Part 1, Part 2 partial, Post Game)\n- Chapter 64T: 2 files (Part 1, Part 2)\n- Chapter 65D: 3 files (Part 1, Part 2, Aftermath)\n- Chapter 65T, 66D: 1 each\n- \"In the Presence of Ghosts\": 1 WAV\n\n**Per recording:**\n```bash\nwhisper --model large-v3 --language en --output_format txt \\\n  \"/path/to/recording.m4a\" -o mardov-vault/sources/transcriptions/\n```\n\n### Task 12: Integrate transcriptions into chapter pages\n\nLink transcriptions to corresponding chapter pages. Extract:\n- Dialogue quotes attributed to characters\n- In-character moments, key decisions\n- DM narration passages\n\n---\n\n## Phase 5: External Source Re-Scrape (Tasks 13-15)\n\n### Task 13: D&D Beyond character re-export\n\n**Requires:** CobaltSession cookie (user pastes into credentials.md)\n\n```bash\npython3 tools/ddb-homebrew-scrape.py --cookie-file /Volumes/synologyNFS/server-manager/secrets/ddb-cobalt-session.txt\n```\n\nExport → `mardov-vault/sources/ddb-export/`\nThen enrich PC pages with stats, inventory, spells.\n\n### Task 14: Roll20 campaign re-export\n\n**Requires:** User runs Roll20 Campaign Exporter Chrome extension\nOutput: 4+ GB ZIP → extract to `sources/roll20-export/`\n\nKey data:\n- Chat logs (actual gameplay dialogue)\n- Journal entries\n- Maps (high-res)\n- Character data\n\n### Task 15: Discord commissioned art scrape\n\n**Requires:** Discord MCP access to Audrey's commission channel\n\nScrape → `assets/pc-art/` organized by character\nExtract character descriptions from chat context → enrich character pages\n\n---\n\n## Phase 6: Synthesis + Cross-Linking (Tasks 16-18)\n\n### Task 16: Master timeline\n\n**Dispatch:** 1 fleet agent (opus for quality)\n\nRead ALL chapter pages + backstories + DM notes → produce:\n- `lore/timeline.md`: chronological events from pre-history through Ch 101+\n- `[[wikilinks]]` to every chapter, character, location mentioned\n- Act/chapter markers for narrative structure\n\n### Task 17: Cosmology + world-building synthesis\n\n**Dispatch:** 1 fleet agent\n\nFrom DM notes, chapter content, NPC data → produce:\n- `lore/cosmology.md`: Gods, Progenitors, Guard'Daemons, planes\n- `lore/magic-system.md`: enriched with chapter examples\n- Update faction pages with cosmological context\n\n### Task 18: Cross-reference validation\n\n**Dispatch:** 1 fleet agent\n\n- Grep ALL `[[wikilinks]]` across vault\n- Find broken links (referenced but no page exists) → create stubs\n- Find orphan pages (exist but nothing links to them) → add links\n- Produce `MOC.md` update with link counts and completeness stats\n\n---\n\n## Phase 7: Production Layer (Tasks 19-20)\n\n### Task 19: Production documents\n\nCreate from existing lessons + vault content:\n- `production/north-star.md`: creative direction rules (from lessons)\n- `production/visual-style-guide.md`: dual-style system, VE post-processing\n- `production/glossary.md`: D&D → Mardov term mapping\n- `production/character-designs/`: per-character visual spec linking to art\n\n### Task 20: Music + panel asset registration\n\nWhen music/panels are regenerated:\n- Register in `assets/music/` and `assets/panels/`\n- Link from chapter pages: \"Scene music: `[[assets/music/track-name.wav]]`\"\n- Link panels to shot sheets\n\n---\n\n## Execution Order\n\n```\nPhase 1 (local, blocking):     Tasks 1-3    ~30 min\nPhase 2 (fleet, parallel):     Tasks 4-6    ~2-3 hours\nPhase 3 (fleet, parallel):     Tasks 7-10   ~2-3 hours (can overlap Phase 2)\nPhase 4 (GPU, sequential):     Tasks 11-12  ~1-2 hours\nPhase 5 (user + scrape):       Tasks 13-15  Blocked on user credentials\nPhase 6 (fleet, sequential):   Tasks 16-18  ~1-2 hours (needs Phase 2-4 done)\nPhase 7 (local):               Tasks 19-20  ~30 min\n```\n\n**Critical path:** Phase 1 → Phase 2+3 (parallel) → Phase 6 → Phase 7\n**Independent:** Phase 4 (can run anytime), Phase 5 (blocked on user)\n\n## Wikilink Convention\n\n- Characters: `[[Daina Fireforge]]`, `[[Sprocket Overspark]]`\n- Locations: `[[Argos]]`, `[[Vag'Mahar]]`, `[[Bynar]]`\n- Chapters: `[[Chapter 0]]`, `[[Chapter 65D]]`\n- Factions: `[[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]]`, `[[Vecna Sworn]]`\n- Items: `[[Ghoul's Fang]]`, `[[Soul Bag]]`\n- Lore: `[[Spell Plague]]`, `[[Guard'Daemons]]`, `[[Progenitors]]`\n\nAliases in frontmatter handle variations:\n```yaml\naliases: [\"Diesa Fireforge\", \"Diesa\", \"Daina\"]\n```\n\n## DM Corrections (from lessons)\n\nThese MUST be applied during extraction:\n- **Finkle = Gnome** (NOT Goblin) — was wrong in 30+ files last time\n- **Sprocket = Gnome** (commission saying Goblin was wrong)\n- **Stalwart = Gold Dragonborn**\n- **Reynaldine (Fall Feylord) ≠ Reynardine (Nyx's patron)** — distinct entities\n- \"Things evolve in a 136-chapter campaign — default to 'it changed' rather than 'someone got it wrong'\"\n","numLines":492,"startLine":1,"totalLines":492}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3a6d0bb4-ad16-4a09-ba9f-69a7099b3077","userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"00fff2bb-a8cc-4993-aadf-6662019d001b","version":"2.1.77","gitBranch":"main","slug":"buzzing-swinging-map"}

/Users/eviemini/.claude/projects/-Users-eviemini-Documents-code-server-manager/00fff2bb-a8cc-4993-aadf-6662019d001b.jsonl:{"parentUuid":"5ff2e609-0e4d-4936-b3e4-d4f7bb065e35","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"f897fb7f-29e5-4b08-ba30-2742e326949a","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01M1VktdPM39NmvnLXjfYPJp","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→# Lessons Index\n     2→\n     3→One-liner per lesson. Read this first, then fetch the relevant topic file.\n     4→\n     5→## MCP (`lessons/mcp.md`)\n     6→- stdout pollution breaks MCP stdio transport — use console.error() only\n     7→- Discord MCP token must be prefixed with \"Bot \" manually\n     8→- Use fetch MCP for HTTP calls, not raw curl\n     9→- **lgtv MCP**: `node --import tsx` fails in Claude Code — use `node_modules/.bin/tsx` binary directly\n    10→- **grafana MCP**: env var is `GRAFANA_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_TOKEN`, not `GRAFANA_TOKEN`\n    11→- **NFS-backed fleet reminders** — JSONL on NFS, shell scripts for create/resolve/check, integrated into session-start + watchdog hooks\n    12→- **SSH proxy pattern for MCP** — thin SSH wrapper script bridges remote Node.js services as local MCP tools\n    13→- **Queue schema mismatch** — verify field names/ID formats match before merging batch items; double-concat corrupts prompts\n    14→- **Three-tier agent memory** — fast cache (seconds) + working memory (minutes) + long-term (cron); NEVER block on cache\n    15→- **Model routing budget control** — opus for creative, sonnet for fleet, haiku for bulk; 40x cost diff; guardrails at 70%/90%\n    16→- **Parallel tool call failure cascading** — if any tool in a parallel batch fails, all siblings cancelled; isolate risky calls\n    17→- **MCP permission wildcards** — new servers need `mcp__servername__*` in settings.json allow list\n    18→- **LG TV MCP fails when TVs off** — pokemote connects via WebSocket at startup; all TVs off = zero tools registered\n    19→- **Synology MCP create_file is text-only** — no binary uploads; use SCP/NFS/API for WAV/images\n    20→- **fetch MCP --ignore-robots-txt** — required for self-hosted services that serve robots.txt\n    21→- **Prep daemon checkpoint quality** — mechanical extraction + LLM-powered fast cache; three-tier fallback\n    22→- **Orphaned log collector** — `tools/collect-orphaned-log.sh` extracts post-prep messages from JSONL transcripts\n    23→\n    24→## Home Assistant (`lessons/ha.md`)\n    25→- Cross-subnet discovery fails — HA can't find IoT devices without second NIC\n    26→- HAOS won't boot with UEFI Secure Boot enabled (pre-enrolled-keys=1)\n    27→- Proxmox API import-from silently fails on xz-compressed qcow2\n    28→- HA REST API: use Bearer token with curl (fetch MCP lacks auth headers)\n    29→- Integration pairing: all headless via config flow API except Plex (needs OAuth)\n    30→- Cross-subnet fix: add net1 on vmbr1 to HA VM, no VLAN tag needed\n    31→- Ollama subentries: use config subentries API for conversation agents\n    32→- **Wyoming config flow**: REST API only — `POST /api/config/config_entries/flow` then submit host/port\n    33→- **Config entry delete**: `DELETE /api/config/config_entries/entry/<id>` (WebSocket `config_entries/delete` = unknown_command)\n    34→- **HA webOS phantom wakes** — webostv reconnection triggers Quick Start+, causing 4-20s wake bounces; built tv-wake-guard.py + TV Guard MCP\n    35→- **HA Ollama think:true breaks llama3.1** — HA 2026.2 sends think:true to non-supporting models; recreate subentry with think:false\n    36→- **HA config entry disable = WebSocket only** — REST API doesn't support it; use WebSocket messages\n    37→- **HA internal URL must match Sonos subnet** — TTS audio on wrong subnet silently fails\n    38→- **Ollama keep_alive:-1** — prevents cold starts; keeps model in VRAM permanently (~80s to ~3s)\n    39→- **Chrome blocks mic on HTTP** — use chrome://flags insecure-origin whitelist or HA Companion app\n    40→\n    41→## Docker (`lessons/docker.md`)\n    42→- **CRITICAL: NEVER deploy fresh containers over existing Plex config — back up Preferences.xml first, use tools/media-deploy.sh**\n    43→- **CRITICAL: NEVER run `docker compose down -v` on media stack — `-v` deletes bind-mounted config data**\n    44→- Plex MachineIdentifier is irreversible (SHA1 hash) — loss = all shared users disconnected permanently\n    45→- Homepage config: write via Synology MCP, can't verify via fetch (Next.js SPA)\n    46→- Portainer admin timeout: recreate container if init window missed\n    47→- Uptime Kuma uses socket.io, not REST — must use web UI for initial setup\n    48→- Container Manager port conflicts: DSM reserves 80/443, use alternates\n    49→- **GPU node (192.168.1.67) ≠ media-server VM 200 (192.168.1.200)** — GPU workloads go to bare-metal GPU node, media stack is on VM 200 with NO GPU\n    50→- Docker on ZFS: requires \"storage-driver\": \"zfs\" in daemon.json\n    51→- WSL2 Docker IPv6 binding: ports bind to [::1] only, use v4tov6 portproxy\n    52→- WSL2 + no-systemd: use /etc/wsl.conf [boot] command= to auto-start services\n    53→- WSL2 from SSH: hangs in session 0 — use Windows Scheduled Task with /ru INTERACTIVE\n    54→- WSL2 LxssManager stuck STOP_PENDING: use WMI Terminate (taskkill/Stop-Process fail even as admin)\n    55→- **SLOW LESSON**: WSL over SSH is a trap — always use the Scheduled Task playbook from the start, never debug iteratively. See docker.md for full playbook.\n    56→- **WSL2 E_FAIL after OOM**: `CreateInstance/E_FAIL` error code 6 = full Windows reboot required. Service restarts (vmcompute, LxssManager) don't fix it. After reboot, fix portproxy manually.\n    57→- **WSL2 Docker bridge subnet overlap**: Default docker0 (172.17.0.0/16) overlaps WSL2 NAT (172.17.192.0/20) — containers can't reach internet. Fix: `\"bip\": \"172.20.0.1/16\"` in daemon.json.\n    58→- **WSL2 SSH on port 2222**: Install OpenSSH inside WSL2, run on 2222. `ssh -p 2222 root@192.168.0.121` — direct Linux shell, no more Scheduled Task wrappers. PERMANENTLY solves session 0 problem.\n    59→- **WSL2 portproxy boot script**: IP changes every restart. `C:\\scripts\\wsl-portproxy.ps1` as startup Scheduled Task refreshes all rules. Ports: 2222, 8188, 11434, 2375.\n    60→- **WSL2 systemd=true**: Replaces fragile `[boot] command=`. `systemctl enable docker ssh` for persistent services. Needs WSL restart to activate.\n    61→- **WSL2 line endings**: Scripts from PowerShell/CMD have CRLF → `$'\\r': command not found` in bash. Use dos2unix or Linux CLI tools on Windows.\n    62→- **WSL2 comprehensive guide**: `docs/services/wsl2-remote-management.md` — SSH, portproxy, Docker TCP, systemd, mirrored mode, WinRM. Needs Windows reboot. New subnet: 192.168.x.x not 172.17.x.x.\n    65→- **WSL2 sshd + systemd conflict**: boot script manually starting sshd + systemd sshd = port conflict → systemd hangs → all services die. Fix: let systemd be the ONLY service manager.\n    66→- **WSL2 PowerShell via SSH**: execution policy blocks .ps1 scripts. double-concat corrupts prompts\n- **Three-tier agent memory** — fast cache (seconds) + working memory (minutes) + long-term (cron); NEVER block on cache\n- **Model routing budget control** — opus for creative, sonnet for fleet, haiku for bulk; 40x cost diff; guardrails at 70%/90%\n- **Parallel tool call failure cascading** — if any tool in a parallel batch fails, all siblings cancelled; isolate risky calls\n- **MCP permission wildcards** — new servers need `mcp__servername__*` in settings.json allow list\n- **LG TV MCP fails when TVs off** — pokemote connects via WebSocket at startup; all TVs off = zero tools registered\n- **Synology MCP create_file is text-only** — no binary uploads; use SCP/NFS/API for WAV/images\n- **fetch MCP --ignore-robots-txt** — required for self-hosted services that serve robots.txt\n- **Prep daemon checkpoint quality** — mechanical extraction + LLM-powered fast cache; three-tier fallback\n- **Orphaned log collector** — `tools/collect-orphaned-log.sh` extracts post-prep messages from JSONL transcripts\n\n## Home Assistant (`lessons/ha.md`)\n- Cross-subnet discovery fails — HA can't find IoT devices without second NIC\n- HAOS won't boot with UEFI Secure Boot enabled (pre-enrolled-keys=1)\n- Proxmox API import-from silently fails on xz-compressed qcow2\n- HA REST API: use Bearer token with curl (fetch MCP lacks auth headers)\n- Integration pairing: all headless via config flow API except Plex (needs OAuth)\n- Cross-subnet fix: add net1 on vmbr1 to HA VM, no VLAN tag needed\n- Ollama subentries: use config subentries API for conversation agents\n- **Wyoming config flow**: REST API only — `POST /api/config/config_entries/flow` then submit host/port\n- **Config entry delete**: `DELETE /api/config/config_entries/entry/<id>` (WebSocket `config_entries/delete` = unknown_command)\n- **HA webOS phantom wakes** — webostv reconnection triggers Quick Start+, causing 4-20s wake bounces; built tv-wake-guard.py + TV Guard MCP\n- **HA Ollama think:true breaks llama3.1** — HA 2026.2 sends think:true to non-supporting models; recreate subentry with think:false\n- **HA config entry disable = WebSocket only** — REST API doesn't support it; use WebSocket messages\n- **HA internal URL must match Sonos subnet** — TTS audio on wrong subnet silently fails\n- **Ollama keep_alive:-1** — prevents cold starts; keeps model in VRAM permanently (~80s to ~3s)\n- **Chrome blocks mic on HTTP** — use chrome://flags insecure-origin whitelist or HA Companion app\n\n## Docker (`lessons/docker.md`)\n- **CRITICAL: NEVER deploy fresh containers over existing Plex config — back up Preferences.xml first, use tools/media-deploy.sh**\n- **CRITICAL: NEVER run `docker compose down -v` on media stack — `-v` deletes bind-mounted config data**\n- Plex MachineIdentifier is irreversible (SHA1 hash) — loss = all shared users disconnected permanently\n- Homepage config: write via Synology MCP, can't verify via fetch (Next.js SPA)\n- Portainer admin timeout: recreate container if init window missed\n- Uptime Kuma uses socket.io, not REST — must use web UI for initial setup\n- Container Manager port conflicts: DSM reserves 80/443, use alternates\n- **GPU node (192.168.1.67) ≠ media-server VM 200 (192.168.1.200)** — GPU workloads go to bare-metal GPU node, media stack is on VM 200 with NO GPU\n- Docker on ZFS: requires \"storage-driver\": \"zfs\" in daemon.json\n- WSL2 Docker IPv6 binding: ports bind to [::1] only, use v4tov6 portproxy\n- WSL2 + no-systemd: use /etc/wsl.conf [boot] command= to auto-start services\n- WSL2 from SSH: hangs in session 0 — use Windows Scheduled Task with /ru INTERACTIVE\n- WSL2 LxssManager stuck STOP_PENDING: use WMI Terminate (taskkill/Stop-Process fail even as admin)\n- **SLOW LESSON**: WSL over SSH is a trap — always use the Scheduled Task playbook from the start, never debug iteratively. See docker.md for full playbook.\n- **WSL2 E_FAIL after OOM**: `CreateInstance/E_FAIL` error code 6 = full Windows reboot required. Service restarts (vmcompute, LxssManager) don't fix it. After reboot, fix portproxy manually.\n- **WSL2 Docker bridge subnet overlap**: Default docker0 (172.17.0.0/16) overlaps WSL2 NAT (172.17.192.0/20) — containers can't reach internet. Fix: `\"bip\": \"172.20.0.1/16\"` in daemon.json.\n- **WSL2 SSH on port 2222**: Install OpenSSH inside WSL2, run on 2222. `ssh -p 2222 root@192.168.0.121` — direct Linux shell, no more Scheduled Task wrappers. PERMANENTLY solves session 0 problem.\n- **WSL2 portproxy boot script**: IP changes every restart. `C:\\scripts\\wsl-portproxy.ps1` as startup Scheduled Task refreshes all rules. Ports: 2222, 8188, 11434, 2375.\n- **WSL2 systemd=true**: Replaces fragile `[boot] command=`. `systemctl enable docker ssh` for persistent services. Needs WSL restart to activate.\n- **WSL2 line endings**: Scripts from PowerShell/CMD have CRLF → `$'\\r': command not found` in bash. Use dos2unix or Linux CLI tools on Windows.\n- **WSL2 comprehensive guide**: `docs/services/wsl2-remote-management.md` — SSH, portproxy, Docker TCP, systemd, mirrored mode, WinRM. Tiny is Win 10 = no mirrored mode.\n- **WSL2 Whisper/GPU gotchas**: stop ComfyUI before GPU work, no `set -e` with torch, NFS `noauto` drops, yt-dlp HLS→NFS stalls (download local first)\n- **WSL2 Store upgrade on Win10**: inbox WSL has no systemd. `wsl --update --web-download` upgrades to Store version (kernel 5.10→6.6). Needs Windows reboot. New subnet: 192.168.x.x not 172.17.x.x.\n- **WSL2 sshd + systemd conflict**: boot script manually starting sshd + systemd sshd = port conflict → systemd hangs → all services die. Fix: let systemd be the ONLY service manager.\n- **WSL2 PowerShell via SSH**: execution policy blocks .ps1 scripts. Use inline netsh commands or `-ExecutionPolicy Bypass`.\n- **WSL2 pipe through Windows SSH**: `wsl -e bash -c 'cmd | grep'` fails — Windows intercepts pipe. Use simple one-liner commands or SSH directly into WSL port 2222.\n- **MusicGen/audiocraft Docker**: PyAV can't compile from source — use `--only-binary=av`. Never conda in pytorch images (upgrades torch). Use `--no-deps audiocraft` + manual deps. Pin torch ecosystem + `transformers==4.36.0` for torch 2.1.x.\n- **Heredocs in Proxmox web console corrupt** — web shell mangles heredoc syntax; use vim or scripts instead\n- **Docker NVIDIA pin GPU** — NVIDIA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 or =1 to pin containers to specific GPUs\n- **WSL2 systemd crash loop on Win10 19045** — crashes in ~4min loop; disable systemd, use manual boot script with nohup/disown\n- **MusicGen cfg_coef typo** — audiocraft uses `cfg_coef` (one f), not `cfg_coeff`; needs explicit static file serving\n\n## Proxmox (`lessons/proxmox.md`)\n- LXC containers broken — always use QEMU VMs\n- SSH to pve node broken — use MCP; gpu node SSH works with proxmox_management key\n- SSH agent keys don't persist across sessions — run `ssh-add` before SSH ops\n- EnterWorktree tool: creates isolated git worktree in .claude/worktrees/\n- **Bulk VM ops (clone/resize/configure) take too long via MCP from Mac** — delegate to a VM agent with direct `qm` CLI access or SSH to PVE. 133 MCP calls = 16 min; same work via CLI = ~2 min.\n- **D-state from dead NFS blocks everything** — `pvestatd` stuck → VM starts fail, `reboot` hangs. Fix: SysRq `s/u/b` forced reboot. Prevention: use `soft` NFS mounts.\n- Clone locks: after `qm clone`, VMs get `lock=clone` in config. Need `qm unlock <vmid>` via SSH to PVE to clear.\n- ipconfig1 leftover: clones from dual-NIC VMs inherit `ipconfig1: ip=dhcp` — cloud-init hangs waiting for missing NIC. Fix: `qm set <vmid> --delete ipconfig1` before first boot.\n- **Claude Code onboarding wizard on headless VMs** — set `hasCompletedOnboarding: true` + `theme: \"dark\"` in `~/.claude.json` to skip browser-based wizard\n- **VM static IPs via netplan** — write static config to `/etc/netplan/50-cloud-init.yaml`, apply with `sudo netplan apply`\n- **`exec claude --resume` kills SSH** — `exec` replaces shell, so `|| fallback` never runs if claude exits. Don't exec the first attempt.\n- **Fleet auth env var override** — `CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN` in `/etc/claude-code.env` overrides credential file. Never hardcode OAuth tokens in env files; use `~/.claude/.credentials.json` + `fleet-reauth.sh`.\n- **NFS symlinks must be relative** — Mac creates `/Volumes/...` absolute symlinks that break on VMs (`/mnt/...`). Always `ln -sf <filename>` (relative).\n- **Hardened launch scripts** — `set -euo pipefail` in `start-claude.sh` kills sessions on any non-critical failure. Only auth should be fatal; NFS/checkpoint/info = best-effort.\n- **Session hook pattern** — SessionStart hook verifies directive signatures + loads latest from NFS; UserPromptSubmit watchdog re-checks mid-session. Agents always see current directives.\n- **LoRA training triple-bug** — accelerate `gpu_ids` must be string \"0\" not int, dataset symlinks must match actual NFS mount path, `shuffle_caption` conflicts with `cache_text_encoder_outputs`\n- **Fleet shared context pattern** — all agents read shared source material before producing role-specific outputs; re-read spec each session to prevent drift. Phase 1 (parallel) then Phase 2 (after human review). Non-overlapping output dirs.\n- **Fleet dispatch vs local subagents** — use fleet (nod/SSH) for VM ops tasks, local subagents for research only. Local agents can't reach VMs and duplicate orchestrator work.\n- **GPU passthrough: remove x-vga=1 for headless** — causes VBIOS init issues; use pcie=1 only for compute VMs\n- **hostpci0 requires session-based root auth** — API tokens can't set PCI passthrough; use web console shell\n- **Fleet background agents die on session reset** — run_in_background agents die on /clear; use nod queue for critical work\n\n## Discord (`lessons/discord.md`)\n- Agent SDK nesting: bot refuses to spawn inside Claude Code session\n- Discord relay: use DM relay for physical pairing tasks (Apple TV PINs, etc.)\n- DAVE protocol: must install `@snazzah/davey` for discord.js voice to work\n- @discordjs/opus: CJS module, use default import in ESM projects\n- launchd EHOSTUNREACH: node under launchd can't reach IoT subnet — run from terminal as workaround\n- **macOS TCC blocks launchd from ~/Documents/** — move services to ~/opt/ or other non-protected dirs\n- .env with unquoted spaces breaks `source .env` under `set -e` — use dotenv in Node instead\n- **Midjourney via Discord relay** — `midjourney` npm package (erictik), user token auth, 1 concurrent + 10s delay, API-agnostic interface\n- **PostToolUse hook for Discord relay** — checks discord-queue.md after tool calls; must escape newlines in JSON output\n- **SessionStart/End hooks for lock file** — auto touch/rm .claude-active for relay/autonomous mode switching\n- **Discord voice messages need OGG Opus + flags=8192** — WAV not playable inline; need OGG Opus, waveform + duration metadata\n\n## Network (`lessons/network.md`)\n- UniFi MCP: basic read tools work, write tools don't — use web UI for config\n- HA → server subnet routing: HAOS default route goes through IoT interface\n- socat proxy on edna-core as routing workaround for cross-subnet\n- **Inter-subnet routing broken**: AT&T BGW320 (192.168.1.254) drops ALL traffic to 192.168.0.x (no route to UDM Pro). Fix: static route `ip route add 192.168.0.0/24 via 192.168.1.91` on each VM, or dual-home with vmbr1.\n- **AT&T BGW320 topology** — NAS/Proxmox on 192.168.1.x bypass UniFi entirely; Synology Router at .64 is transparent bridge\n- **Tailscale IP for local+remote** — routes locally on LAN, tunnels when remote; single address for service configs\n- **Cross-subnet file transfer** — Mac on IoT can't SCP to 1.x directly; use intermediate dual-homed node as relay\n\n## Voice / Edna (`lessons/voice.md`)\n- Guest agent exec: MCP blocks special chars, no shell pipes/redirects\n- Ollama needs $HOME set — guest agent exec doesn't set shell env\n- HA → Ollama: use IoT subnet IP (192.168.0.210), server subnet path hangs\n- Ollama Assist API: 163 entities makes prompt too large for 8B model on CPU\n- Cloud image clone: netplan has MAC-matched IPs, clone gets new MACs → no network\n- apt restricted repo: Ubuntu 22.04 has corrupt nvidia metadata, remove restricted\n- GPU Xid 79: GPU fell off PCIe bus, requires full node reboot\n- XTTS Wyoming: voice files in /data/voices/, scan_voices() only at container start\n- Kokoro GPU image `latest` dropped Pascal support (CUDA 12.9) — pin `v0.2.1` for 1080 Ti/1060\n- openWakeWord can't train full models on macOS (needs Piper/Linux) — use CoreWorxLab Docker trainer\n- Custom wake word: 16+ real voice samples → Kokoro TTS generates ~13K synthetic → train on GPU\n- Store training data on NFS for retraining (17GB ACAV100M features + AudioSet + FMA)\n- Kokoro TTS segfaults under WSL2 memory pressure — add circuit breaker, restart policy, shm_size\n- Trainer service needs `shm_size: '2g'` — PyTorch DataLoader workers crash with Bus error on Docker's default 64MB\n- CheckTraining scheduled task can reference stale container names — validate container is running, not just that logs exist\n- Writing files to WSL2 over SSH: scp to C:\\temp then wsl cp (pipes don't work through Windows CMD)\n- Phantom scheduled tasks: 37 leftover Oww* schtasks auto-launched trainers — always clean up after debugging\n- Trainer Dockerfile has no CMD: need `command:` in compose, not just `docker compose run trainer`\n- **Wyoming openWakeWord only loads TFLite** — `.onnx` files silently ignored (no onnxruntime in image)\n- **ONNX→TFLite**: use `onnx2tf` without `-oiqt` (INT8 fails on 3D tensor); float32 output works\n- **Wyoming HA integration**: use REST API for config flow (WebSocket lacks delete/flow commands)\n- **Wyoming protocol**: header JSON + raw data bytes (not a single JSON response)\n- **wake_word entity \"unknown\"**: normal state before first detection — not an error\n- **Edna Mode voiced by Brad Bird** — not Linda Hunt; use Incredibles movie dialogue for voice cloning\n- **XTTS multi-file: single combined WAV** — multiple clips hang; single 10-30s WAV works reliably\n- **Chatterbox Turbo replaced XTTS v2** — better quality, MIT license, ~4.8GB VRAM, EXAGGERATION param + [laugh] tags\n- **Demucs for vocal isolation** — Meta's model strips vocals from background music/SFX; run on GPU node\n- **Chatterbox EXAG+CFG tuning** — EXAG 1.0=max drama, 0.25-0.6 calmer; CFG constrains to reference tone\n\n## Media / Indexers (`lessons/media.md`)\n- NZBGeek $12/yr: best value indexer, 11yr retention, unlimited API on paid tier, native Prowlarr def\n- Prowlarr MCP search returns partial results — Sonarr/Radarr query Prowlarr directly and work fine\n- Survivor S50E01: DMCA takedowns hit all usenet releases within hours — more indexers = better odds\n- SceneNZBs \"free\" API is actually a 30-day trial, not permanent\n- Tabula-Rasa registration window closes without warning — register immediately when open\n- **RECURRING**: Usenet auth failures — DON'T assume expired accounts. Check SABnzbd config against NFS secrets file (passwords may be wrong). Usenet Agency `482 Account expired` = generic auth rejection.\n- SABnzbd API: use `/api?mode=...` (not `/sabnzbd/api`), requires `apikey=` param\n- VM 200 at 94.7% RAM: guest agent dies, use SABnzbd HTTP API or Proxmox reboot as fallback\n- Radarr empty placeholder dirs are NOT files — RescanMovie won't help, use MoviesSearch to trigger downloads\n- Sonarr import blocked = check NAS dir ownership (UID mismatch prevents mkdir)\n- Radarr bulk import via API: lookup by TMDB, POST /api/v3/movie, RescanMovie to link files\n- VaultBoi1 (/movies) is read-only to Radarr — new downloads go to /movies2\n- macOS BSD grep lacks -P (PCRE) — use sed in scripts that run on Mac\n- NFS unzip freezes on large archives — extract locally then rsync to NFS\n- UniFi API 403 on network config mods — must use web UI for WAN/Internet changes\n- WAN2 flapping breaks WebSocket apps (Venmo) — disable Internet 2 in UniFi UI\n- **CRITICAL — Mardov creative adaptation**: DM is source material, not production manager. Players are primary audience. Ensemble — every player gets seen. Produce complete work → DM gives notes → iterate. Read `NORTH_STAR.md` before any Mardov work.\n- **LoRA training is wrong tool for early art direction** — lock visual style BEFORE training character models. LoRAs with bad training data + wrong base model = awful output. MJ `--cref`/`--sref` > LoRAs for MJ-primary pipeline. Be specific in style tags (name shows/studios, not \"Gothic anime\").\n- **Tiny WSL2 remote management** — SSH to port 2222 for direct Linux shell. Docker localhost broken, use container IP. OOM kills WSL entirely. WSL-Watchdog scheduled task for auto-recovery.\n- **Kling Playwright automation** — `storage_state` cookies don't persist auth; use `launch_persistent_context` with `~/.kling-profile/`. Login detection: \"Sign In\" text absence = logged in (Generate button visible even when logged out). Navigate to generation page for auth gate, not main feed. Videos use `blob:` URLs — intercept CDN URLs from API responses via `page.on(\"response\")`.\n- **CRITICAL — Kling i2v upload silently fails** — `input[type=\"file\"]` selector broke when Kling UI updated. Upload skips silently, Kling falls back to text-only generation. ALL 92 videos were generic t2v, not image-seeded. ALWAYS test one i2v manually before batch runs. Fix the selector first, verify the MJ source image appears in the UI, THEN batch.\n- **Kling download validation** — API 500 errors get saved as .mp4 files (399 bytes of JSON). Added validation: reject files <100KB + verify MP4 ftyp magic bytes. Always check `kling-old/` for backup copies before regenerating.\n- **MJ hopper websocket hang** — illustrator.js hangs indefinitely when MJ websocket drops silently. Added `timeout 900` wrapper. Monitor batch progress — if no log activity for 15+ min, the illustrator is stuck.\n- **CRITICAL — Discord selfbot websocket MESSAGE filtering** — Discord now suppresses MESSAGE_CREATE/MESSAGE_UPDATE events on selfbot gateway connections. MJ Imagine commands send fine (INTERACTION_SUCCESS) but results never arrive via websocket. Fix: HTTP poll `GET /channels/{id}/messages` every 5s. Deployed `illustrator-poll.js` on VM 150.\n- **CRITICAL — Kling URL capture grabs wrong videos** — `_extract_urls()` grabbed ANY .mp4 URL from ANY API response, including other users' videos in the feed gallery. All 92 downloads were random videos. Fix: capture task ID from `task/submit` response, only accept URLs from responses containing that task ID. Also: stale results file fallback in mj-hopper.sh read old batch-results — must compare against pre-existing file.\n- **CRITICAL — MJ hopper ban** — 154 batches with no backoff (770 requests, 59% fail rate) triggered account ban. Rewrite with exponential backoff, daily cap 50-75, ban detection, `--relax` mode default, review checkpoint every 10 panels\n- **MJ --no + --niji 7 conflict** — `--no` parameter is incompatible with `--niji 7` mode. Returns \"Invalid parameter\". Strip `--no` segments from niji prompts.\n- **MJ anime style transfer** — use `--sref` not blend, `--oref` not `--cref` in V7, `/describe` first, `--stylize 50-75` not `--style raw` (does nothing in V7)\n- **Fleet-wide data corrections** — systematic grep ALL production files when canonical error found; one attribute wrong in 30+ places\n- **LoRA base model compat** — verify SD1.5 vs SDXL before using; SDXL LoRAs incompatible with SD1.5 AnimateDiff\n- **Kling billing** — API=$4,200 min vs $26/mo Pro web UI; cookie replay fails (anti-bot signing); Playwright for web automation\n- **Plex library path removal is metadata-only** — PUT with paths-to-keep, not DELETE; safe, doesn't touch files\n- **Plex movie count drop = missing NFS mount** — removed paths + Docker only mapped one NAS volume = 337 movies vanished\n- **LunaSea discontinued** — use Helmarr (iOS) or nzb360 (Android) for mobile media management\n- **Duplicate movie cleanup** — delete all inferior copies first for max free space, then batch-move better copies\n- **NFS v4.1 upgrade is config-only** — enable in Synology DSM + change fstab to nfs4 vers=4.1\n- **MJ Hopper NFS results detection fragile** — NFS caching misses files; parse results path from illustrator output\n- **MJ Hopper crash leaves status.json stale** — add on-exit trap to reset \"generating\" items to \"pending\"\n- **MJ Hopper stale lockfile** — validate PID against running processes before blocking restart\n- **Sonarr anime naming** — anime releases use bare episode numbers; set series type to \"anime\"\n- **Sonarr cutoff blocking re-grabs** — failed download history blocks new grabs; clear blocklist for fresh grabs\n- **ComfyUI custom_nodes path mismatch** — verify correct path relative to app directory\n- **Wan 2.1 text encoder corrupt** — 439MB vs expected ~4.9GB; always verify model file sizes after download\n- **IP-Adapter + ControlNet VE style transfer** — SD 1.5 + IP-Adapter 0.3-0.6 + ControlNet Canny, denoise 0.25-0.40\n- **Production notes as canonical source of truth** — timestamped notes in designated dir with INDEX tracking OPEN/CLOSED\n- **Lovable UI selector breaks** — changed textarea to TipTap contenteditable div; always inspect target UI before batch runs\n- **Roll20 S3 URLs expire** — signed tokens expire; use Campaign Exporter Chrome extension\n- **DDB API: CobaltSession cookie** — `character-service.dndbeyond.com/character/v5/character/{ID}` with CobaltSession cookie\n- **yt-dlp HLS stalls on NFS** — HLS fragment downloads over NFS extremely slow; download to /tmp first, then copy\n- **MJ void prompt** — aggressive `--no` for minimalist compositions; low `--stylize` for raw output\n\n## Gemini API / Model Routing (`lessons/media.md`)\n- **Gemini as coding agent**: keep tasks tiny (1 file change), send complete files not diffs, watch for truncation on 500+ line files\n- **gemini-code.sh pattern**: generate→apply→build→fix loop, auto-restores backups on failure\n- **Model availability**: 2.5 Flash works reliably; 2.0 Flash Lite quota takes hours to provision on new projects; 3.1 Flash Lite gets overloaded easily\n- **Import mutation**: Gemini sometimes changes unrelated imports (e.g. @dnd-kit/sortable → @dnd-kit/core) — always diff-review before committing\n\n## MCP — Gemini/Model Routing (`lessons/mcp.md`)\n- **Gemini API quota delay on new projects** — 2.0 Flash Lite takes hours to provision; use 2.5 Flash while waiting\n- **Gemini truncates long files + mutates imports** — truncates at ~500 lines; mutates unrelated imports; always diff-review\n\n## Storage / NAS (`lessons/storage.md`)\n- **CRITICAL — NFS SPOF incident**: server-manager dir replaced with broken symlink, all operational data lost. No snapshots, no backup = no recovery. Install Snapshot Replication + Hyper Backup IMMEDIATELY. Full plan: `docs/recovery/RECOVERY-PLAN.md`\n- Synology MCP = FileStation only — query DSM API directly for storage health (SYNO.Storage.CGI.Storage)\n- Synology \"attention\" status = capacity warning (fs_almost_full), not disk failure\n- SHR pools spanning expansion units = eSATA SPOF — isolate each DX517 as its own pool\n- TrueNAS VM 104 uses physical passthrough via /dev/disk/by-id/ paths, not virtual disks\n- PVE hardware discovery: /nodes/pve/disks/list + /nodes/pve/hardware/pci via API token auth\n- TrueNAS Scratch degradation: ZFS metadata corruption from unclean shutdowns, not disk failure\n- **Persistent data must NOT live on Scratch** — moved to Synology SHR-2. Scratch = downloads/temp ONLY. Path mapping in `tool-guide.md`.\n- **macOS NFS mount gotchas**: `mapall` is NOT valid on macOS. UID 501≠1000 = write denied. Fix: Synology DSM squash rules PLUS chown to tyler:administrators (squash maps to admin UID 1026, not file owner UID 1000). Must remount after. diskutil unmount hangs on busy mounts. NEVER rm -rf mount points. Main=/volume1, VaultBoi1Main=/volume2 (different!).\n- **Synology SSH**: port 420 (non-standard), user tyler. Use `ssh -p 420 tyler@192.168.1.103` for permission fixes that can't be done via MCP (read-only account).\n- **DS1821+ has 4GB RAM min** — supports 32GB; DDR4 ECC SO-DIMMs ~$50-80 for 16GB upgrade\n- **Synology NFS admin = DSM admin account** — mcp-server needs admin for storage/disk/RAID/network APIs (error 105 without)\n- **SMB drive mappings drop across SSH** — Windows maps per-logon-session; use UNC paths in scripts\n- **PowerShell password escaping over SSH** — $, &, ! mangled through bash→ssh→cmd→PS chain; use net use or temp files\n- **Synology SSH admin paths** — sshpass on Mac, sudo -S for stdin password, synoshare at /usr/syno/sbin/, no crontab (edit /etc/crontab), DSM API 403 for share config (use SSH CLI), btrfs snapshots work direct\n- **WSL2 local vs NFS confusion** — /mnt/synology-nfs/server-manager/ on WSL2 is LOCAL storage not NFS-mounted; verify with `mount` command\n- **NFS `noauto` mount drops silently in WSL2** — fstab `noauto` means mounts drop between sessions; remount before each batch\n\n## Hardware / USB (`lessons/hardware.md`)\n- USB 3.0 radiates 2.4GHz RF — interferes with wireless dongles (G733) and USB audio (Yeti)\n- Blue Yeti draws 500mA — passive USB hubs can't power it reliably\n- Fix: dedicated USB 2.0 hub physically separated from USB 3.0 ports\n- Corsair keyboards expose 3-4 HID endpoints — slow KVM re-enumeration, worse with iCUE running\n- Mac Mini has no USB 2.0 ports — need USB 2.0 hub via adapter for audio isolation\n- HA voice satellites: ESP32-S3-BOX-3 supports on-device microWakeWord, Voice PE has echo cancellation\n- **GPU VRAM contention** — profile each service's VRAM; 1080 Ti can't run Chatterbox (6.2GB) + ComfyUI simultaneously; stop one first\n- **PSU wattage caused GPU Xid 79** — 400W insufficient for GTX 1060 120W TDP; upgraded to 850W Segotep GM850\n- **ASRock Z170M Extreme4 dual-GPU layout** — PCIE1+PCIE3 share 16 lanes (x8/x8); 1080 Ti + 1060, PCIE4 for future 10G NIC\n- **DDR4 prices skyrocketed in 2026** — cheapest 2x8GB ~$97 (was $25); AI-driven pricing on EOL DDR4\n- **ComfyUI VAEDecodeTiled for 8GB VRAM** — VAEDecode OOMs at 768x1024+; swap to VAEDecodeTiled (tile_size=512)\n- **WSL2 Store upgrade on Win10** — inbox WSL has no systemd; `wsl --update --web-download` to get Store version; requires reboot\n","numLines":243,"startLine":1,"totalLines":243}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5ff2e609-0e4d-4936-b3e4-d4f7bb065e35","userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"00fff2bb-a8cc-4993-aadf-6662019d001b","version":"2.1.77","gitBranch":"main","slug":"buzzing-swinging-map"}
