WA has internal AJAX APIs for article discovery.","originalFile":"# Lessons Index\n\nOne-liner per lesson. Read this first, then fetch the relevant topic file.\n\n## MCP (`lessons/mcp.md`)\n- stdout pollution breaks MCP stdio transport — use console.error() only\n- Discord MCP token must be prefixed with \"Bot \" manually\n- Use fetch MCP for HTTP calls, not raw curl\n- **lgtv MCP**: `node --import tsx` fails in Claude Code — use `node_modules/.bin/tsx` binary directly\n- **grafana MCP**: env var is `GRAFANA_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_TOKEN`, not `GRAFANA_TOKEN`\n- **NFS-backed fleet reminders** — JSONL on NFS, shell scripts for create/resolve/check, integrated into session-start + watchdog hooks\n- **SSH proxy pattern for MCP** — thin SSH wrapper script bridges remote Node.js services as local MCP tools\n- **Queue schema mismatch** — verify field names/ID formats match before merging batch items; double-concat corrupts prompts\n- **Three-tier agent memory** — fast cache (seconds) + working memory (minutes) + long-term (cron); NEVER block on cache\n- **Model routing budget control** — opus for creative, sonnet for fleet, haiku for bulk; 40x cost diff; guardrails at 70%/90%\n- **Parallel tool call failure cascading** — if any tool in a parallel batch fails, all siblings cancelled; isolate risky calls\n- **MCP permission wildcards** — new servers need `mcp__servername__*` in settings.json allow list\n- **LG TV MCP fails when TVs off** — pokemote connects via WebSocket at startup; all TVs off = zero tools registered\n- **Synology MCP create_file is text-only** — no binary uploads; use SCP/NFS/API for WAV/images\n- **fetch MCP --ignore-robots-txt** — required for self-hosted services that serve robots.txt\n- **Prep daemon checkpoint quality** — mechanical extraction + LLM-powered fast cache; three-tier fallback\n- **Orphaned log collector** — `tools/collect-orphaned-log.sh` extracts post-prep messages from JSONL transcripts\n\n## Home Assistant (`lessons/ha.md`)\n- Cross-subnet discovery fails — HA can't find IoT devices without second NIC\n- HAOS won't boot with UEFI Secure Boot enabled (pre-enrolled-keys=1)\n- Proxmox API import-from silently fails on xz-compressed qcow2\n- HA REST API: use Bearer token with curl (fetch MCP lacks auth headers)\n- Integration pairing: all headless via config flow API except Plex (needs OAuth)\n- Cross-subnet fix: add net1 on vmbr1 to HA VM, no VLAN tag needed\n- Ollama subentries: use config subentries API for conversation agents\n- **Wyoming config flow**: REST API only — `POST /api/config/config_entries/flow` then submit host/port\n- **Config entry delete**: `DELETE /api/config/config_entries/entry/<id>` (WebSocket `config_entries/delete` = unknown_command)\n- **HA webOS phantom wakes** — webostv reconnection triggers Quick Start+, causing 4-20s wake bounces; built tv-wake-guard.py + TV Guard MCP\n- **HA Ollama think:true breaks llama3.1** — HA 2026.2 sends think:true to non-supporting models; recreate subentry with think:false\n- **HA config entry disable = WebSocket only** — REST API doesn't support it; use WebSocket messages\n- **HA internal URL must match Sonos subnet** — TTS audio on wrong subnet silently fails\n- **Ollama keep_alive:-1** — prevents cold starts; keeps model in VRAM permanently (~80s to ~3s)\n- **Chrome blocks mic on HTTP** — use chrome://flags insecure-origin whitelist or HA Companion app\n\n## Docker (`lessons/docker.md`)\n- **CRITICAL: NEVER deploy fresh containers over existing Plex config — back up Preferences.xml first, use tools/media-deploy.sh**\n- **CRITICAL: NEVER run `docker compose down -v` on media stack — `-v` deletes bind-mounted config data**\n- Plex MachineIdentifier is irreversible (SHA1 hash) — loss = all shared users disconnected permanently\n- Homepage config: write via Synology MCP, can't verify via fetch (Next.js SPA)\n- Portainer admin timeout: recreate container if init window missed\n- Uptime Kuma uses socket.io, not REST — must use web UI for initial setup\n- Container Manager port conflicts: DSM reserves 80/443, use alternates\n- **GPU node (192.168.1.67) ≠ media-server VM 200 (192.168.1.200)** — GPU workloads go to bare-metal GPU node, media stack is on VM 200 with NO GPU\n- Docker on ZFS: requires \"storage-driver\": \"zfs\" in daemon.json\n- WSL2 Docker IPv6 binding: ports bind to [::1] only, use v4tov6 portproxy\n- WSL2 + no-systemd: use /etc/wsl.conf [boot] command= to auto-start services\n- WSL2 from SSH: hangs in session 0 — use Windows Scheduled Task with /ru INTERACTIVE\n- WSL2 LxssManager stuck STOP_PENDING: use WMI Terminate (taskkill/Stop-Process fail even as admin)\n- **SLOW LESSON**: WSL over SSH is a trap — always use the Scheduled Task playbook from the start, never debug iteratively. See docker.md for full playbook.\n- **WSL2 E_FAIL after OOM**: `CreateInstance/E_FAIL` error code 6 = full Windows reboot required. Service restarts (vmcompute, LxssManager) don't fix it. After reboot, fix portproxy manually.\n- **WSL2 Docker bridge subnet overlap**: Default docker0 (172.17.0.0/16) overlaps WSL2 NAT (172.17.192.0/20) — containers can't reach internet. Fix: `\"bip\": \"172.20.0.1/16\"` in daemon.json.\n- **WSL2 SSH on port 2222**: Install OpenSSH inside WSL2, run on 2222. `ssh -p 2222 root@192.168.0.121` — direct Linux shell, no more Scheduled Task wrappers. PERMANENTLY solves session 0 problem.\n- **WSL2 portproxy boot script**: IP changes every restart. `C:\\scripts\\wsl-portproxy.ps1` as startup Scheduled Task refreshes all rules. Ports: 2222, 8188, 11434, 2375.\n- **WSL2 systemd=true**: Replaces fragile `[boot] command=`. `systemctl enable docker ssh` for persistent services. Needs WSL restart to activate.\n- **WSL2 line endings**: Scripts from PowerShell/CMD have CRLF → `$'\\r': command not found` in bash. Use dos2unix or Linux CLI tools on Windows.\n- **WSL2 comprehensive guide**: `docs/services/wsl2-remote-management.md` — SSH, portproxy, Docker TCP, systemd, mirrored mode, WinRM. Tiny is Win 10 = no mirrored mode.\n- **WSL2 Whisper/GPU gotchas**: stop ComfyUI before GPU work, no `set -e` with torch, NFS `noauto` drops, yt-dlp HLS→NFS stalls (download local first)\n- **WSL2 Store upgrade on Win10**: inbox WSL has no systemd. `wsl --update --web-download` upgrades to Store version (kernel 5.10→6.6). Needs Windows reboot. New subnet: 192.168.x.x not 172.17.x.x.\n- **WSL2 sshd + systemd conflict**: boot script manually starting sshd + systemd sshd = port conflict → systemd hangs → all services die. Fix: let systemd be the ONLY service manager.\n- **WSL2 PowerShell via SSH**: execution policy blocks .ps1 scripts. Use inline netsh commands or `-ExecutionPolicy Bypass`.\n- **WSL2 pipe through Windows SSH**: `wsl -e bash -c 'cmd | grep'` fails — Windows intercepts pipe. Use simple one-liner commands or SSH directly into WSL port 2222.\n- **MusicGen/audiocraft Docker**: PyAV can't compile from source — use `--only-binary=av`. Never conda in pytorch images (upgrades torch). Use `--no-deps audiocraft` + manual deps. Pin torch ecosystem + `transformers==4.36.0` for torch 2.1.x.\n- **Heredocs in Proxmox web console corrupt** — web shell mangles heredoc syntax; use vim or scripts instead\n- **Docker NVIDIA pin GPU** — NVIDIA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 or =1 to pin containers to specific GPUs\n- **WSL2 systemd crash loop on Win10 19045** — crashes in ~4min loop; disable systemd, use manual boot script with nohup/disown\n- **MusicGen cfg_coef typo** — audiocraft uses `cfg_coef` (one f), not `cfg_coeff`; needs explicit static file serving\n\n## Proxmox (`lessons/proxmox.md`)\n- LXC containers broken — always use QEMU VMs\n- SSH to pve node broken — use MCP; gpu node SSH works with proxmox_management key\n- SSH agent keys don't persist across sessions — run `ssh-add` before SSH ops\n- EnterWorktree tool: creates isolated git worktree in .claude/worktrees/\n- **Bulk VM ops (clone/resize/configure) take too long via MCP from Mac** — delegate to a VM agent with direct `qm` CLI access or SSH to PVE. 133 MCP calls = 16 min; same work via CLI = ~2 min.\n- **D-state from dead NFS blocks everything** — `pvestatd` stuck → VM starts fail, `reboot` hangs. Fix: SysRq `s/u/b` forced reboot. Prevention: use `soft` NFS mounts.\n- Clone locks: after `qm clone`, VMs get `lock=clone` in config. Need `qm unlock <vmid>` via SSH to PVE to clear.\n- ipconfig1 leftover: clones from dual-NIC VMs inherit `ipconfig1: ip=dhcp` — cloud-init hangs waiting for missing NIC. Fix: `qm set <vmid> --delete ipconfig1` before first boot.\n- **Claude Code onboarding wizard on headless VMs** — set `hasCompletedOnboarding: true` + `theme: \"dark\"` in `~/.claude.json` to skip browser-based wizard\n- **VM static IPs via netplan** — write static config to `/etc/netplan/50-cloud-init.yaml`, apply with `sudo netplan apply`\n- **`exec claude --resume` kills SSH** — `exec` replaces shell, so `|| fallback` never runs if claude exits. Don't exec the first attempt.\n- **Fleet auth env var override** — `CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN` in `/etc/claude-code.env` overrides credential file. Never hardcode OAuth tokens in env files; use `~/.claude/.credentials.json` + `fleet-reauth.sh`.\n- **NFS symlinks must be relative** — Mac creates `/Volumes/...` absolute symlinks that break on VMs (`/mnt/...`). Always `ln -sf <filename>` (relative).\n- **Hardened launch scripts** — `set -euo pipefail` in `start-claude.sh` kills sessions on any non-critical failure. Only auth should be fatal; NFS/checkpoint/info = best-effort.\n- **Session hook pattern** — SessionStart hook verifies directive signatures + loads latest from NFS; UserPromptSubmit watchdog re-checks mid-session. Agents always see current directives.\n- **LoRA training triple-bug** — accelerate `gpu_ids` must be string \"0\" not int, dataset symlinks must match actual NFS mount path, `shuffle_caption` conflicts with `cache_text_encoder_outputs`\n- **Fleet shared context pattern** — all agents read shared source material before producing role-specific outputs; re-read spec each session to prevent drift. Phase 1 (parallel) then Phase 2 (after human review). Non-overlapping output dirs.\n- **Fleet dispatch vs local subagents** — use fleet (nod/SSH) for VM ops tasks, local subagents for research only. Local agents can't reach VMs and duplicate orchestrator work.\n- **GPU passthrough: remove x-vga=1 for headless** — causes VBIOS init issues; use pcie=1 only for compute VMs\n- **hostpci0 requires session-based root auth** — API tokens can't set PCI passthrough; use web console shell\n- **Fleet background agents die on session reset** — run_in_background agents die on /clear; use nod queue for critical work\n\n## Discord (`lessons/discord.md`)\n- Agent SDK nesting: bot refuses to spawn inside Claude Code session\n- Discord relay: use DM relay for physical pairing tasks (Apple TV PINs, etc.)\n- DAVE protocol: must install `@snazzah/davey` for discord.js voice to work\n- @discordjs/opus: CJS module, use default import in ESM projects\n- launchd EHOSTUNREACH: node under launchd can't reach IoT subnet — run from terminal as workaround\n- **macOS TCC blocks launchd from ~/Documents/** — move services to ~/opt/ or other non-protected dirs\n- .env with unquoted spaces breaks `source .env` under `set -e` — use dotenv in Node instead\n- **Midjourney via Discord relay** — `midjourney` npm package (erictik), user token auth, 1 concurrent + 10s delay, API-agnostic interface\n- **PostToolUse hook for Discord relay** — checks discord-queue.md after tool calls; must escape newlines in JSON output\n- **SessionStart/End hooks for lock file** — auto touch/rm .claude-active for relay/autonomous mode switching\n- **Discord voice messages need OGG Opus + flags=8192** — WAV not playable inline; need OGG Opus, waveform + duration metadata\n\n## Network (`lessons/network.md`)\n- UniFi MCP: basic read tools work, write tools don't — use web UI for config\n- HA → server subnet routing: HAOS default route goes through IoT interface\n- socat proxy on edna-core as routing workaround for cross-subnet\n- **Inter-subnet routing broken**: AT&T BGW320 (192.168.1.254) drops ALL traffic to 192.168.0.x (no route to UDM Pro). Fix: static route `ip route add 192.168.0.0/24 via 192.168.1.91` on each VM, or dual-home with vmbr1.\n- **AT&T BGW320 topology** — NAS/Proxmox on 192.168.1.x bypass UniFi entirely; Synology Router at .64 is transparent bridge\n- **Tailscale IP for local+remote** — routes locally on LAN, tunnels when remote; single address for service configs\n- **Cross-subnet file transfer** — Mac on IoT can't SCP to 1.x directly; use intermediate dual-homed node as relay\n\n## Voice / Edna (`lessons/voice.md`)\n- Guest agent exec: MCP blocks special chars, no shell pipes/redirects\n- Ollama needs $HOME set — guest agent exec doesn't set shell env\n- HA → Ollama: use IoT subnet IP (192.168.0.210), server subnet path hangs\n- Ollama Assist API: 163 entities makes prompt too large for 8B model on CPU\n- Cloud image clone: netplan has MAC-matched IPs, clone gets new MACs → no network\n- apt restricted repo: Ubuntu 22.04 has corrupt nvidia metadata, remove restricted\n- GPU Xid 79: GPU fell off PCIe bus, requires full node reboot\n- XTTS Wyoming: voice files in /data/voices/, scan_voices() only at container start\n- Kokoro GPU image `latest` dropped Pascal support (CUDA 12.9) — pin `v0.2.1` for 1080 Ti/1060\n- openWakeWord can't train full models on macOS (needs Piper/Linux) — use CoreWorxLab Docker trainer\n- Custom wake word: 16+ real voice samples → Kokoro TTS generates ~13K synthetic → train on GPU\n- Store training data on NFS for retraining (17GB ACAV100M features + AudioSet + FMA)\n- Kokoro TTS segfaults under WSL2 memory pressure — add circuit breaker, restart policy, shm_size\n- Trainer service needs `shm_size: '2g'` — PyTorch DataLoader workers crash with Bus error on Docker's default 64MB\n- CheckTraining scheduled task can reference stale container names — validate container is running, not just that logs exist\n- Writing files to WSL2 over SSH: scp to C:\\temp then wsl cp (pipes don't work through Windows CMD)\n- Phantom scheduled tasks: 37 leftover Oww* schtasks auto-launched trainers — always clean up after debugging\n- Trainer Dockerfile has no CMD: need `command:` in compose, not just `docker compose run trainer`\n- **Wyoming openWakeWord only loads TFLite** — `.onnx` files silently ignored (no onnxruntime in image)\n- **ONNX→TFLite**: use `onnx2tf` without `-oiqt` (INT8 fails on 3D tensor); float32 output works\n- **Wyoming HA integration**: use REST API for config flow (WebSocket lacks delete/flow commands)\n- **Wyoming protocol**: header JSON + raw data bytes (not a single JSON response)\n- **wake_word entity \"unknown\"**: normal state before first detection — not an error\n- **Edna Mode voiced by Brad Bird** — not Linda Hunt; use Incredibles movie dialogue for voice cloning\n- **XTTS multi-file: single combined WAV** — multiple clips hang; single 10-30s WAV works reliably\n- **Chatterbox Turbo replaced XTTS v2** — better quality, MIT license, ~4.8GB VRAM, EXAGGERATION param + [laugh] tags\n- **Demucs for vocal isolation** — Meta's model strips vocals from background music/SFX; run on GPU node\n- **Chatterbox EXAG+CFG tuning** — EXAG 1.0=max drama, 0.25-0.6 calmer; CFG constrains to reference tone\n\n## Media / Indexers (`lessons/media.md`)\n- NZBGeek $12/yr: best value indexer, 11yr retention, unlimited API on paid tier, native Prowlarr def\n- Prowlarr MCP search returns partial results — Sonarr/Radarr query Prowlarr directly and work fine\n- Survivor S50E01: DMCA takedowns hit all usenet releases within hours — more indexers = better odds\n- SceneNZBs \"free\" API is actually a 30-day trial, not permanent\n- Tabula-Rasa registration window closes without warning — register immediately when open\n- **RECURRING**: Usenet auth failures — DON'T assume expired accounts. Check SABnzbd config against NFS secrets file (passwords may be wrong). Usenet Agency `482 Account expired` = generic auth rejection.\n- SABnzbd API: use `/api?mode=...` (not `/sabnzbd/api`), requires `apikey=` param\n- VM 200 at 94.7% RAM: guest agent dies, use SABnzbd HTTP API or Proxmox reboot as fallback\n- Radarr empty placeholder dirs are NOT files — RescanMovie won't help, use MoviesSearch to trigger downloads\n- Sonarr import blocked = check NAS dir ownership (UID mismatch prevents mkdir)\n- Radarr bulk import via API: lookup by TMDB, POST /api/v3/movie, RescanMovie to link files\n- VaultBoi1 (/movies) is read-only to Radarr — new downloads go to /movies2\n- macOS BSD grep lacks -P (PCRE) — use sed in scripts that run on Mac\n- NFS unzip freezes on large archives — extract locally then rsync to NFS\n- UniFi API 403 on network config mods — must use web UI for WAN/Internet changes\n- WAN2 flapping breaks WebSocket apps (Venmo) — disable Internet 2 in UniFi UI\n- **CRITICAL — Mardov creative adaptation**: DM is source material, not production manager. Players are primary audience. Ensemble — every player gets seen. Produce complete work → DM gives notes → iterate. Read `NORTH_STAR.md` before any Mardov work.\n- **LoRA training is wrong tool for early art direction** — lock visual style BEFORE training character models. LoRAs with bad training data + wrong base model = awful output. MJ `--cref`/`--sref` > LoRAs for MJ-primary pipeline. Be specific in style tags (name shows/studios, not \"Gothic anime\").\n- **Tiny WSL2 remote management** — SSH to port 2222 for direct Linux shell. Docker localhost broken, use container IP. OOM kills WSL entirely. WSL-Watchdog scheduled task for auto-recovery.\n- **Kling Playwright automation** — `storage_state` cookies don't persist auth; use `launch_persistent_context` with `~/.kling-profile/`. Login detection: \"Sign In\" text absence = logged in (Generate button visible even when logged out). Navigate to generation page for auth gate, not main feed. Videos use `blob:` URLs — intercept CDN URLs from API responses via `page.on(\"response\")`.\n- **CRITICAL — Kling i2v upload silently fails** — `input[type=\"file\"]` selector broke when Kling UI updated. Upload skips silently, Kling falls back to text-only generation. ALL 92 videos were generic t2v, not image-seeded. ALWAYS test one i2v manually before batch runs. Fix the selector first, verify the MJ source image appears in the UI, THEN batch.\n- **Kling download validation** — API 500 errors get saved as .mp4 files (399 bytes of JSON). Added validation: reject files <100KB + verify MP4 ftyp magic bytes. Always check `kling-old/` for backup copies before regenerating.\n- **MJ hopper websocket hang** — illustrator.js hangs indefinitely when MJ websocket drops silently. Added `timeout 900` wrapper. Monitor batch progress — if no log activity for 15+ min, the illustrator is stuck.\n- **CRITICAL — Discord selfbot websocket MESSAGE filtering** — Discord now suppresses MESSAGE_CREATE/MESSAGE_UPDATE events on selfbot gateway connections. MJ Imagine commands send fine (INTERACTION_SUCCESS) but results never arrive via websocket. Fix: HTTP poll `GET /channels/{id}/messages` every 5s. Deployed `illustrator-poll.js` on VM 150.\n- **CRITICAL — Kling URL capture grabs wrong videos** — `_extract_urls()` grabbed ANY .mp4 URL from ANY API response, including other users' videos in the feed gallery. All 92 downloads were random videos. Fix: capture task ID from `task/submit` response, only accept URLs from responses containing that task ID. Also: stale results file fallback in mj-hopper.sh read old batch-results — must compare against pre-existing file.\n- **CRITICAL — MJ hopper ban** — 154 batches with no backoff (770 requests, 59% fail rate) triggered account ban. Rewrite with exponential backoff, daily cap 50-75, ban detection, `--relax` mode default, review checkpoint every 10 panels\n- **MJ --no + --niji 7 conflict** — `--no` parameter is incompatible with `--niji 7` mode. Returns \"Invalid parameter\". Strip `--no` segments from niji prompts.\n- **MJ anime style transfer** — use `--sref` not blend, `--oref` not `--cref` in V7, `/describe` first, `--stylize 50-75` not `--style raw` (does nothing in V7)\n- **Fleet-wide data corrections** — systematic grep ALL production files when canonical error found; one attribute wrong in 30+ places\n- **LoRA base model compat** — verify SD1.5 vs SDXL before using; SDXL LoRAs incompatible with SD1.5 AnimateDiff\n- **Kling billing** — API=$4,200 min vs $26/mo Pro web UI; cookie replay fails (anti-bot signing); Playwright for web automation\n- **Plex library path removal is metadata-only** — PUT with paths-to-keep, not DELETE; safe, doesn't touch files\n- **Plex movie count drop = missing NFS mount** — removed paths + Docker only mapped one NAS volume = 337 movies vanished\n- **LunaSea discontinued** — use Helmarr (iOS) or nzb360 (Android) for mobile media management\n- **Duplicate movie cleanup** — delete all inferior copies first for max free space, then batch-move better copies\n- **NFS v4.1 upgrade is config-only** — enable in Synology DSM + change fstab to nfs4 vers=4.1\n- **MJ Hopper NFS results detection fragile** — NFS caching misses files; parse results path from illustrator output\n- **MJ Hopper crash leaves status.json stale** — add on-exit trap to reset \"generating\" items to \"pending\"\n- **MJ Hopper stale lockfile** — validate PID against running processes before blocking restart\n- **Sonarr anime naming** — anime releases use bare episode numbers; set series type to \"anime\"\n- **Sonarr cutoff blocking re-grabs** — failed download history blocks new grabs; clear blocklist for fresh grabs\n- **ComfyUI custom_nodes path mismatch** — verify correct path relative to app directory\n- **Wan 2.1 text encoder corrupt** — 439MB vs expected ~4.9GB; always verify model file sizes after download\n- **IP-Adapter + ControlNet VE style transfer** — SD 1.5 + IP-Adapter 0.3-0.6 + ControlNet Canny, denoise 0.25-0.40\n- **Production notes as canonical source of truth** — timestamped notes in designated dir with INDEX tracking OPEN/CLOSED\n- **Lovable UI selector breaks** — changed textarea to TipTap contenteditable div; always inspect target UI before batch runs\n- **Roll20 S3 URLs expire** — signed tokens expire; use Campaign Exporter Chrome extension\n- **DDB API: CobaltSession cookie** — `character-service.dndbeyond.com/character/v5/character/{ID}` with CobaltSession cookie\n- **yt-dlp HLS stalls on NFS** — HLS fragment downloads over NFS extremely slow; download to /tmp first, then copy\n- **MJ void prompt** — aggressive `--no` for minimalist compositions; low `--stylize` for raw output\n\n## Gemini API / Model Routing (`lessons/media.md`)\n- **Gemini as coding agent**: keep tasks tiny (1 file change), send complete files not diffs, watch for truncation on 500+ line files\n- **gemini-code.sh pattern**: generate→apply→build→fix loop, auto-restores backups on failure\n- **Model availability**: 2.5 Flash works reliably; 2.0 Flash Lite quota takes hours to provision on new projects; 3.1 Flash Lite gets overloaded easily\n- **Import mutation**: Gemini sometimes changes unrelated imports (e.g. @dnd-kit/sortable → @dnd-kit/core) — always diff-review before committing\n\n## MCP — Gemini/Model Routing (`lessons/mcp.md`)\n- **Gemini API quota delay on new projects** — 2.0 Flash Lite takes hours to provision; use 2.5 Flash while waiting\n- **Gemini truncates long files + mutates imports** — truncates at ~500 lines; mutates unrelated imports; always diff-review\n\n## Storage / NAS (`lessons/storage.md`)\n- **CRITICAL — NFS SPOF incident**: server-manager dir replaced with broken symlink, all operational data lost. No snapshots, no backup = no recovery. Install Snapshot Replication + Hyper Backup IMMEDIATELY. Full plan: `docs/recovery/RECOVERY-PLAN.md`\n- Synology MCP = FileStation only — query DSM API directly for storage health (SYNO.Storage.CGI.Storage)\n- Synology \"attention\" status = capacity warning (fs_almost_full), not disk failure\n- SHR pools spanning expansion units = eSATA SPOF — isolate each DX517 as its own pool\n- TrueNAS VM 104 uses physical passthrough via /dev/disk/by-id/ paths, not virtual disks\n- PVE hardware discovery: /nodes/pve/disks/list + /nodes/pve/hardware/pci via API token auth\n- TrueNAS Scratch degradation: ZFS metadata corruption from unclean shutdowns, not disk failure\n- **Persistent data must NOT live on Scratch** — moved to Synology SHR-2. Scratch = downloads/temp ONLY. Path mapping in `tool-guide.md`.\n- **macOS NFS mount gotchas**: `mapall` is NOT valid on macOS. UID 501≠1000 = write denied. Fix: Synology DSM squash rules PLUS chown to tyler:administrators (squash maps to admin UID 1026, not file owner UID 1000). Must remount after. diskutil unmount hangs on busy mounts. NEVER rm -rf mount points. Main=/volume1, VaultBoi1Main=/volume2 (different!).\n- **Synology SSH**: port 420 (non-standard), user tyler. Use `ssh -p 420 tyler@192.168.1.103` for permission fixes that can't be done via MCP (read-only account).\n- **DS1821+ has 4GB RAM min** — supports 32GB; DDR4 ECC SO-DIMMs ~$50-80 for 16GB upgrade\n- **Synology NFS admin = DSM admin account** — mcp-server needs admin for storage/disk/RAID/network APIs (error 105 without)\n- **SMB drive mappings drop across SSH** — Windows maps per-logon-session; use UNC paths in scripts\n- **PowerShell password escaping over SSH** — $, &, ! mangled through bash→ssh→cmd→PS chain; use net use or temp files\n- **Synology SSH admin paths** — sshpass on Mac, sudo -S for stdin password, synoshare at /usr/syno/sbin/, no crontab (edit /etc/crontab), DSM API 403 for share config (use SSH CLI), btrfs snapshots work direct\n- **WSL2 local vs NFS confusion** — /mnt/synology-nfs/server-manager/ on WSL2 is LOCAL storage not NFS-mounted; verify with `mount` command\n- **NFS `noauto` mount drops silently in WSL2** — fstab `noauto` means mounts drop between sessions; remount before each batch\n\n## Hardware / USB (`lessons/hardware.md`)\n- USB 3.0 radiates 2.4GHz RF — interferes with wireless dongles (G733) and USB audio (Yeti)\n- Blue Yeti draws 500mA — passive USB hubs can't power it reliably\n- Fix: dedicated USB 2.0 hub physically separated from USB 3.0 ports\n- Corsair keyboards expose 3-4 HID endpoints — slow KVM re-enumeration, worse with iCUE running\n- Mac Mini has no USB 2.0 ports — need USB 2.0 hub via adapter for audio isolation\n- HA voice satellites: ESP32-S3-BOX-3 supports on-device microWakeWord, Voice PE has echo cancellation\n- **GPU VRAM contention** — profile each service's VRAM; 1080 Ti can't run Chatterbox (6.2GB) + ComfyUI simultaneously; stop one first\n- **PSU wattage caused GPU Xid 79** — 400W insufficient for GTX 1060 120W TDP; upgraded to 850W Segotep GM850\n- **ASRock Z170M Extreme4 dual-GPU layout** — PCIE1+PCIE3 share 16 lanes (x8/x8); 1080 Ti + 1060, PCIE4 for future 10G NIC\n- **DDR4 prices skyrocketed in 2026** — cheapest 2x8GB ~$97 (was $25); AI-driven pricing on EOL DDR4\n- **ComfyUI VAEDecodeTiled for 8GB VRAM** — VAEDecode OOMs at 768x1024+; swap to VAEDecodeTiled (tile_size=512)\n- **WSL2 Store upgrade on Win10** — inbox WSL has no systemd; `wsl --update --web-download` to get Store version; requires reboot\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":115,"oldLines":6,"newStart":115,"newLines":7,"lines":[" - **AT&T BGW320 topology** — NAS/Proxmox on 192.168.1.x bypass UniFi entirely; Read this first, then fetch the relevant topic file.\n\n## MCP (`lessons/mcp.md`)\n- stdout pollution breaks MCP stdio transport — use console.error() only\n- Discord MCP token must be prefixed with \"Bot \" manually\n- Use fetch MCP for HTTP calls, not raw curl\n- **lgtv MCP**: `node --import tsx` fails in Claude Code — use `node_modules/.bin/tsx` binary directly\n- **grafana MCP**: env var is `GRAFANA_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_TOKEN`, not `GRAFANA_TOKEN`\n- **NFS-backed fleet reminders** — JSONL on NFS, shell scripts for create/resolve/check, integrated into session-start + watchdog hooks\n- **SSH proxy pattern for MCP** — thin SSH wrapper script bridges remote Node.js services as local MCP tools\n- **Queue schema mismatch** — verify field names/ID formats match before merging batch items; double-concat corrupts prompts\n- **Three-tier agent memory** — fast cache (seconds) + working memory (minutes) + long-term (cron); NEVER block on cache\n- **Model routing budget control** — opus for creative, sonnet for fleet, haiku for bulk; 40x cost diff; guardrails at 70%/90%\n- **Parallel tool call failure cascading** — if any tool in a parallel batch fails, all siblings cancelled; isolate risky calls\n- **MCP permission wildcards** — new servers need `mcp__servername__*` in settings.json allow list\n- **LG TV MCP fails when TVs off** — pokemote connects via WebSocket at startup; all TVs off = zero tools registered\n- **Synology MCP create_file is text-only** — no binary uploads; use SCP/NFS/API for WAV/images\n- **fetch MCP --ignore-robots-txt** — required for self-hosted services that serve robots.txt\n- **Prep daemon checkpoint quality** — mechanical extraction + LLM-powered fast cache; three-tier fallback\n- **Orphaned log collector** — `tools/collect-orphaned-log.sh` extracts post-prep messages from JSONL transcripts\n\n## Home Assistant (`lessons/ha.md`)\n- Cross-subnet discovery fails — HA can't find IoT devices without second NIC\n- HAOS won't boot with UEFI Secure Boot enabled (pre-enrolled-keys=1)\n- Proxmox API import-from silently fails on xz-compressed qcow2\n- HA REST API: use Bearer token with curl (fetch MCP lacks auth headers)\n- Integration pairing: all headless via config flow API except Plex (needs OAuth)\n- Cross-subnet fix: add net1 on vmbr1 to HA VM, no VLAN tag needed\n- Ollama subentries: use config subentries API for conversation agents\n- **Wyoming config flow**: REST API only — `POST /api/config/config_entries/flow` then submit host/port\n- **Config entry delete**: `DELETE /api/config/config_entries/entry/<id>` (WebSocket `config_entries/delete` = unknown_command)\n- **HA webOS phantom wakes** — webostv reconnection triggers Quick Start+, causing 4-20s wake bounces; built tv-wake-guard.py + TV Guard MCP\n- **HA Ollama think:true breaks llama3.1** — HA 2026.2 sends think:true to non-supporting models; recreate subentry with think:false\n- **HA config entry disable = WebSocket only** — REST API doesn't support it; use WebSocket messages\n- **HA internal URL must match Sonos subnet** — TTS audio on wrong subnet silently fails\n- **Ollama keep_alive:-1** — prevents cold starts; keeps model in VRAM permanently (~80s to ~3s)\n- **Chrome blocks mic on HTTP** — use chrome://flags insecure-origin whitelist or HA Companion app\n\n## Docker (`lessons/docker.md`)\n- **CRITICAL: NEVER deploy fresh containers over existing Plex config — back up Preferences.xml first, use tools/media-deploy.sh**\n- **CRITICAL: NEVER run `docker compose down -v` on media stack — `-v` deletes bind-mounted config data**\n- Plex MachineIdentifier is irreversible (SHA1 hash) — loss = all shared users disconnected permanently\n- Homepage config: write via Synology MCP, can't verify via fetch (Next.js SPA)\n- Portainer admin timeout: recreate container if init window missed\n- Uptime Kuma uses socket.io, not REST — must use web UI for initial setup\n- Container Manager port conflicts: DSM reserves 80/443, use alternates\n- **GPU node (192.168.1.67) ≠ media-server VM 200 (192.168.1.200)** — GPU workloads go to bare-metal GPU node, media stack is on VM 200 with NO GPU\n- Docker on ZFS: requires \"storage-driver\": \"zfs\" in daemon.json\n- WSL2 Docker IPv6 binding: ports bind to [::1] only, use v4tov6 portproxy\n- WSL2 + no-systemd: use /etc/wsl.conf [boot] command= to auto-start services\n- WSL2 from SSH: hangs in session 0 — use Windows Scheduled Task with /ru INTERACTIVE\n- WSL2 LxssManager stuck STOP_PENDING: use WMI Terminate (taskkill/Stop-Process fail even as admin)\n- **SLOW LESSON**: WSL over SSH is a trap — always use the Scheduled Task playbook from the start, never debug iteratively. See docker.md for full playbook.\n- **WSL2 E_FAIL after OOM**: `CreateInstance/E_FAIL` error code 6 = full Windows reboot required. Service restarts (vmcompute, LxssManager) don't fix it. After reboot, fix portproxy manually.\n- **WSL2 Docker bridge subnet overlap**: Default docker0 (172.17.0.0/16) overlaps WSL2 NAT (172.17.192.0/20) — containers can't reach internet. Fix: `\"bip\": \"172.20.0.1/16\"` in daemon.json.\n- **WSL2 SSH on port 2222**: Install OpenSSH inside WSL2, run on 2222. `ssh -p 2222 root@192.168.0.121` — direct Linux shell, no more Scheduled Task wrappers. PERMANENTLY solves session 0 problem.\n- **WSL2 portproxy boot script**: IP changes every restart. `C:\\scripts\\wsl-portproxy.ps1` as startup Scheduled Task refreshes all rules. Ports: 2222, 8188, 11434, 2375.\n- **WSL2 systemd=true**: Replaces fragile `[boot] command=`. `systemctl enable docker ssh` for persistent services. Needs WSL restart to activate.\n- **WSL2 line endings**: Scripts from PowerShell/CMD have CRLF → `$'\\r': command not found` in bash. Use dos2unix or Linux CLI tools on Windows.\n- **WSL2 comprehensive guide**: `docs/services/wsl2-remote-management.md` — SSH, portproxy, Docker TCP, systemd, mirrored mode, WinRM. Tiny is Win 10 = no mirrored mode.\n- **WSL2 Whisper/GPU gotchas**: stop ComfyUI before GPU work, no `set -e` with torch, NFS `noauto` drops, yt-dlp HLS→NFS stalls (download local first)\n- **WSL2 Store upgrade on Win10**: inbox WSL has no systemd. `wsl --update --web-download` upgrades to Store version (kernel 5.10→6.6). Needs Windows reboot. New subnet: 192.168.x.x not 172.17.x.x.\n- **WSL2 sshd + systemd conflict**: boot script manually starting sshd + systemd sshd = port conflict → systemd hangs → all services die. Fix: let systemd be the ONLY service manager.\n- **WSL2 PowerShell via SSH**: execution policy blocks .ps1 scripts. Use inline netsh commands or `-ExecutionPolicy Bypass`.\n- **WSL2 pipe through Windows SSH**: `wsl -e bash -c 'cmd | grep'` fails — Windows intercepts pipe. Use simple one-liner commands or SSH directly into WSL port 2222.\n- **MusicGen/audiocraft Docker**: PyAV can't compile from source — use `--only-binary=av`. Never conda in pytorch images (upgrades torch). Use `--no-deps audiocraft` + manual deps. Pin torch ecosystem + `transformers==4.36.0` for torch 2.1.x.\n- **Heredocs in Proxmox web console corrupt** — web shell mangles heredoc syntax; use vim or scripts instead\n- **Docker NVIDIA pin GPU** — NVIDIA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 or =1 to pin containers to specific GPUs\n- **WSL2 systemd crash loop on Win10 19045** — crashes in ~4min loop; disable systemd, use manual boot script with nohup/disown\n- **MusicGen cfg_coef typo** — audiocraft uses `cfg_coef` (one f), not `cfg_coeff`; needs explicit static file serving\n\n## Proxmox (`lessons/proxmox.md`)\n- LXC containers broken — always use QEMU VMs\n- SSH to pve node broken — use MCP; gpu node SSH works with proxmox_management key\n- SSH agent keys don't persist across sessions — run `ssh-add` before SSH ops\n- EnterWorktree tool: creates isolated git worktree in .claude/worktrees/\n- **Bulk VM ops (clone/resize/configure) take too long via MCP from Mac** — delegate to a VM agent with direct `qm` CLI access or SSH to PVE. 133 MCP calls = 16 min; same work via CLI = ~2 min.\n- **D-state from dead NFS blocks everything** — `pvestatd` stuck → VM starts fail, `reboot` hangs. Fix: SysRq `s/u/b` forced reboot. Prevention: use `soft` NFS mounts.\n- Clone locks: after `qm clone`, VMs get `lock=clone` in config. Need `qm unlock <vmid>` via SSH to PVE to clear.\n- ipconfig1 leftover: clones from dual-NIC VMs inherit `ipconfig1: ip=dhcp` — cloud-init hangs waiting for missing NIC. Fix: `qm set <vmid> --delete ipconfig1` before first boot.\n- **Claude Code onboarding wizard on headless VMs** — set `hasCompletedOnboarding: true` + `theme: \"dark\"` in `~/.claude.json` to skip browser-based wizard\n- **VM static IPs via netplan** — write static config to `/etc/netplan/50-cloud-init.yaml`, apply with `sudo netplan apply`\n- **`exec claude --resume` kills SSH** — `exec` replaces shell, so `|| fallback` never runs if claude exits. Don't exec the first attempt.\n- **Fleet auth env var override** — `CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN` in `/etc/claude-code.env` overrides credential file. Never hardcode OAuth tokens in env files; use `~/.claude/.credentials.json` + `fleet-reauth.sh`.\n- **NFS symlinks must be relative** — Mac creates `/Volumes/...` absolute symlinks that break on VMs (`/mnt/...`). Always `ln -sf <filename>` (relative).\n- **Hardened launch scripts** — `set -euo pipefail` in `start-claude.sh` kills sessions on any non-critical failure. Only auth should be fatal; NFS/checkpoint/info = best-effort.\n- **Session hook pattern** — SessionStart hook verifies directive signatures + loads latest from NFS; UserPromptSubmit watchdog re-checks mid-session. Agents always see current directives.\n- **LoRA training triple-bug** — accelerate `gpu_ids` must be string \"0\" not int, dataset symlinks must match actual NFS mount path, `shuffle_caption` conflicts with `cache_text_encoder_outputs`\n- **Fleet shared context pattern** — all agents read shared source material before producing role-specific outputs; re-read spec each session to prevent drift. Phase 1 (parallel) then Phase 2 (after human review). Non-overlapping output dirs.\n- **Fleet dispatch vs local subagents** — use fleet (nod/SSH) for VM ops tasks, local subagents for research only. Local agents can't reach VMs and duplicate orchestrator work.\n- **GPU passthrough: remove x-vga=1 for headless** — causes VBIOS init issues; use pcie=1 only for compute VMs\n- **hostpci0 requires session-based root auth** — API tokens can't set PCI passthrough; use web console shell\n- **Fleet background agents die on session reset** — run_in_background agents die on /clear; use nod queue for critical work\n\n## Discord (`lessons/discord.md`)\n- Agent SDK nesting: bot refuses to spawn inside Claude Code session\n- Discord relay: use DM relay for physical pairing tasks (Apple TV PINs, etc.)\n- DAVE protocol: must install `@snazzah/davey` for discord.js voice to work\n- @discordjs/opus: CJS module, use default import in ESM projects\n- launchd EHOSTUNREACH: node under launchd can't reach IoT subnet — run from terminal as workaround\n- **macOS TCC blocks launchd from ~/Documents/** — move services to ~/opt/ or other non-protected dirs\n- .env with unquoted spaces breaks `source .env` under `set -e` — use dotenv in Node instead\n- **Midjourney via Discord relay** — `midjourney` npm package (erictik), user token auth, 1 concurrent + 10s delay, API-agnostic interface\n- **PostToolUse hook for Discord relay** — checks discord-queue.md after tool calls; must escape newlines in JSON output\n- **SessionStart/End hooks for lock file** — auto touch/rm .claude-active for relay/autonomous mode switching\n- **Discord voice messages need OGG Opus + flags=8192** — WAV not playable inline; need OGG Opus, waveform + duration metadata\n\n## Network (`lessons/network.md`)\n- UniFi MCP: basic read tools work, write tools don't — use web UI for config\n- HA → server subnet routing: HAOS default route goes through IoT interface\n- socat proxy on edna-core as routing workaround for cross-subnet\n- **Inter-subnet routing broken**: AT&T BGW320 (192.168.1.254) drops ALL traffic to 192.168.0.x (no route to UDM Pro). Fix: static route `ip route add 192.168.0.0/24 via 192.168.1.91` on each VM, or dual-home with vmbr1.\n- **AT&T BGW320 topology** — NAS/Proxmox on 192.168.1.x bypass UniFi entirely; Synology Router at .64 is transparent bridge\n- **Tailscale IP for local+remote** — routes locally on LAN, tunnels when remote; single address for service configs\n- **Cross-subnet file transfer** — Mac on IoT can't SCP to 1.x directly; use intermediate dual-homed node as relay\n- **World Anvil Cloudflare bypass** — Playwright/curl blocked by cf_clearance fingerprinting; use cloudscraper lib or MCP fetch tool. WA has internal AJAX APIs for article discovery.\n\n## Voice / Edna (`lessons/voice.md`)\n- Guest agent exec: MCP blocks special chars, no shell pipes/redirects\n- Ollama needs $HOME set — guest agent exec doesn't set shell env\n- HA → Ollama: use IoT subnet IP (192.168.0.210), server subnet path hangs\n- Ollama Assist API: 163 entities makes prompt too large for 8B model on CPU\n- Cloud image clone: netplan has MAC-matched IPs, clone gets new MACs → no network\n- apt restricted repo: Ubuntu 22.04 has corrupt nvidia metadata, remove restricted\n- GPU Xid 79: GPU fell off PCIe bus, requires full node reboot\n- XTTS Wyoming: voice files in /data/voices/, scan_voices() only at container start\n- Kokoro GPU image `latest` dropped Pascal support (CUDA 12.9) — pin `v0.2.1` for 1080 Ti/1060\n- openWakeWord can't train full models on macOS (needs Piper/Linux) — use CoreWorxLab Docker trainer\n- Custom wake word: 16+ real voice samples → Kokoro TTS generates ~13K synthetic → train on GPU\n- Store training data on NFS for retraining (17GB ACAV100M features + AudioSet + FMA)\n- Kokoro TTS segfaults under WSL2 memory pressure — add circuit breaker, restart policy, shm_size\n- Trainer service needs `shm_size: '2g'` — PyTorch DataLoader workers crash with Bus error on Docker's default 64MB\n- CheckTraining scheduled task can reference stale container names — validate container is running, not just that logs exist\n- Writing files to WSL2 over SSH: scp to C:\\temp then wsl cp (pipes don't work through Windows CMD)\n- Phantom scheduled tasks: 37 leftover Oww* schtasks auto-launched trainers — always clean up after debugging\n- Trainer Dockerfile has no CMD: need `command:` in compose, not just `docker compose run trainer`\n- **Wyoming openWakeWord only loads TFLite** — `.onnx` files silently ignored (no onnxruntime in image)\n- **ONNX→TFLite**: use `onnx2tf` without `-oiqt` (INT8 fails on 3D tensor); float32 output works\n- **Wyoming HA integration**: use REST API for config flow (WebSocket lacks delete/flow commands)\n- **Wyoming protocol**: header JSON + raw data bytes (not a single JSON response)\n- **wake_word entity \"unknown\"**: normal state before first detection — not an error\n- **Edna Mode voiced by Brad Bird** — not Linda Hunt; use Incredibles movie dialogue for voice cloning\n- **XTTS multi-file: single combined WAV** — multiple clips hang; single 10-30s WAV works reliably\n- **Chatterbox Turbo replaced XTTS v2** — better quality, MIT license, ~4.8GB VRAM, EXAGGERATION param + [laugh] tags\n- **Demucs for vocal isolation** — Meta's model strips vocals from background music/SFX; run on GPU node\n- **Chatterbox EXAG+CFG tuning** — EXAG 1.0=max drama, 0.25-0.6 calmer; CFG constrains to reference tone\n\n## Media / Indexers (`lessons/media.md`)\n- NZBGeek $12/yr: best value indexer, 11yr retention, unlimited API on paid tier, native Prowlarr def\n- Prowlarr MCP search returns partial results — Sonarr/Radarr query Prowlarr directly and work fine\n- Survivor S50E01: DMCA takedowns hit all usenet releases within hours — more indexers = better odds\n- SceneNZBs \"free\" API is actually a 30-day trial, not permanent\n- Tabula-Rasa registration window closes without warning — register immediately when open\n- **RECURRING**: Usenet auth failures — DON'T assume expired accounts. Check SABnzbd config against NFS secrets file (passwords may be wrong). Usenet Agency `482 Account expired` = generic auth rejection.\n- SABnzbd API: use `/api?mode=...` (not `/sabnzbd/api`), requires `apikey=` param\n- VM 200 at 94.7% RAM: guest agent dies, use SABnzbd HTTP API or Proxmox reboot as fallback\n- Radarr empty placeholder dirs are NOT files — RescanMovie won't help, use MoviesSearch to trigger downloads\n- Sonarr import blocked = check NAS dir ownership (UID mismatch prevents mkdir)\n- Radarr bulk import via API: lookup by TMDB, POST /api/v3/movie, RescanMovie to link files\n- VaultBoi1 (/movies) is read-only to Radarr — new downloads go to /movies2\n- macOS BSD grep lacks -P (PCRE) — use sed in scripts that run on Mac\n- NFS unzip freezes on large archives — extract locally then rsync to NFS\n- UniFi API 403 on network config mods — must use web UI for WAN/Internet changes\n- WAN2 flapping breaks WebSocket apps (Venmo) — disable Internet 2 in UniFi UI\n- **CRITICAL — Mardov creative adaptation**: DM is source material, not production manager. Players are primary audience. Ensemble — every player gets seen. Produce complete work → DM gives notes → iterate. Read `NORTH_STAR.md` before any Mardov work.\n- **LoRA training is wrong tool for early art direction** — lock visual style BEFORE training character models. LoRAs with bad training data + wrong base model = awful output. MJ `--cref`/`--sref` > LoRAs for MJ-primary pipeline. Be specific in style tags (name shows/studios, not \"Gothic anime\").\n- **Tiny WSL2 remote management** — SSH to port 2222 for direct Linux shell. Docker localhost broken, use container IP. OOM kills WSL entirely. WSL-Watchdog scheduled task for auto-recovery.\n- **Kling Playwright automation** — `storage_state` cookies don't persist auth; use `launch_persistent_context` with `~/.kling-profile/`. Login detection: \"Sign In\" text absence = logged in (Generate button visible even when logged out). Navigate to generation page for auth gate, not main feed. Videos use `blob:` URLs — intercept CDN URLs from API responses via `page.on(\"response\")`.\n- **CRITICAL — Kling i2v upload silently fails** — `input[type=\"file\"]` selector broke when Kling UI updated. Upload skips silently, Kling falls back to text-only generation. ALL 92 videos were generic t2v, not image-seeded. ALWAYS test one i2v manually before batch runs. Fix the selector first, verify the MJ source image appears in the UI, THEN batch.\n- **Kling download validation** — API 500 errors get saved as .mp4 files (399 bytes of JSON). Added validation: reject files <100KB + verify MP4 ftyp magic bytes. Always check `kling-old/` for backup copies before regenerating.\n- **MJ hopper websocket hang** — illustrator.js hangs indefinitely when MJ websocket drops silently. Added `timeout 900` wrapper. Monitor batch progress — if no log activity for 15+ min, the illustrator is stuck.\n- **CRITICAL — Discord selfbot websocket MESSAGE filtering** — Discord now suppresses MESSAGE_CREATE/MESSAGE_UPDATE events on selfbot gateway connections. MJ Imagine commands send fine (INTERACTION_SUCCESS) but results never arrive via websocket. Fix: HTTP poll `GET /channels/{id}/messages` every 5s. Deployed `illustrator-poll.js` on VM 150.\n- **CRITICAL — Kling URL capture grabs wrong videos** — `_extract_urls()` grabbed ANY .mp4 URL from ANY API response, including other users' videos in the feed gallery. All 92 downloads were random videos. Fix: capture task ID from `task/submit` response, only accept URLs from responses containing that task ID. Also: stale results file fallback in mj-hopper.sh read old batch-results — must compare against pre-existing file.\n- **CRITICAL — MJ hopper ban** — 154 batches with no backoff (770 requests, 59% fail rate) triggered account ban. Rewrite with exponential backoff, daily cap 50-75, ban detection, `--relax` mode default, review checkpoint every 10 panels\n- **MJ --no + --niji 7 conflict** — `--no` parameter is incompatible with `--niji 7` mode. Returns \"Invalid parameter\". Strip `--no` segments from niji prompts.\n- **MJ anime style transfer** — use `--sref` not blend, `--oref` not `--cref` in V7, `/describe` first, `--stylize 50-75` not `--style raw` (does nothing in V7)\n- **Fleet-wide data corrections** — systematic grep ALL production files when canonical error found; one attribute wrong in 30+ places\n- **LoRA base model compat** — verify SD1.5 vs SDXL before using; SDXL LoRAs incompatible with SD1.5 AnimateDiff\n- **Kling billing** — API=$4,200 min vs $26/mo Pro web UI; cookie replay fails (anti-bot signing); Playwright for web automation\n- **Plex library path removal is metadata-only** — PUT with paths-to-keep, not DELETE; safe, doesn't touch files\n- **Plex movie count drop = missing NFS mount** — removed paths + Docker only mapped one NAS volume = 337 movies vanished\n- **LunaSea discontinued** — use Helmarr (iOS) or nzb360 (Android) for mobile media management\n- **Duplicate movie cleanup** — delete all inferior copies first for max free space, then batch-move better copies\n- **NFS v4.1 upgrade is config-only** — enable in Synology DSM + change fstab to nfs4 vers=4.1\n- **MJ Hopper NFS results detection fragile** — NFS caching misses files; parse results path from illustrator output\n- **MJ Hopper crash leaves status.json stale** — add on-exit trap to reset \"generating\" items to \"pending\"\n- **MJ Hopper stale lockfile** — validate PID against running processes before blocking restart\n- **Sonarr anime naming** — anime releases use bare episode numbers; set series type to \"anime\"\n- **Sonarr cutoff blocking re-grabs** — failed download history blocks new grabs; clear blocklist for fresh grabs\n- **ComfyUI custom_nodes path mismatch** — verify correct path relative to app directory\n- **Wan 2.1 text encoder corrupt** — 439MB vs expected ~4.9GB; always verify model file sizes after download\n- **IP-Adapter + ControlNet VE style transfer** — SD 1.5 + IP-Adapter 0.3-0.6 + ControlNet Canny, denoise 0.25-0.40\n- **Production notes as canonical source of truth** — timestamped notes in designated dir with INDEX tracking OPEN/CLOSED\n- **Lovable UI selector breaks** — changed textarea to TipTap contenteditable div; always inspect target UI before batch runs\n- **Roll20 S3 URLs expire** — signed tokens expire; use Campaign Exporter Chrome extension\n- **DDB API: CobaltSession cookie** — `character-service.dndbeyond.com/character/v5/character/{ID}` with CobaltSession cookie\n- **yt-dlp HLS stalls on NFS** — HLS fragment downloads over NFS extremely slow; download to /tmp first, then copy\n- **MJ void prompt** — aggressive `--no` for minimalist compositions; low `--stylize` for raw output\n\n## Gemini API / Model Routing (`lessons/media.md`)\n- **Gemini as coding agent**: keep tasks tiny (1 file change), send complete files not diffs, watch for truncation on 500+ line files\n- **gemini-code.sh pattern**: generate→apply→build→fix loop, auto-restores backups on failure\n- **Model availability**: 2.5 Flash works reliably; 2.0 Flash Lite quota takes hours to provision on new projects; 3.1 Flash Lite gets overloaded easily\n- **Import mutation**: Gemini sometimes changes unrelated imports (e.g. @dnd-kit/sortable → @dnd-kit/core) — always diff-review before committing\n\n## MCP — Gemini/Model Routing (`lessons/mcp.md`)\n- **Gemini API quota delay on new projects** — 2.0 Flash Lite takes hours to provision; use 2.5 Flash while waiting\n- **Gemini truncates long files + mutates imports** — truncates at ~500 lines; mutates unrelated imports; always diff-review\n\n## Storage / NAS (`lessons/storage.md`)\n- **CRITICAL — NFS SPOF incident**: server-manager dir replaced with broken symlink, all operational data lost. No snapshots, no backup = no recovery. Install Snapshot Replication + Hyper Backup IMMEDIATELY. Full plan: `docs/recovery/RECOVERY-PLAN.md`\n- Synology MCP = FileStation only — query DSM API directly for storage health (SYNO.Storage.CGI.Storage)\n- Synology \"attention\" status = capacity warning (fs_almost_full), not disk failure\n- SHR pools spanning expansion units = eSATA SPOF — isolate each DX517 as its own pool\n- TrueNAS VM 104 uses physical passthrough via /dev/disk/by-id/ paths, not virtual disks\n- PVE hardware discovery: /nodes/pve/disks/list + /nodes/pve/hardware/pci via API token auth\n- TrueNAS Scratch degradation: ZFS metadata corruption from unclean shutdowns, not disk failure\n- **Persistent data must NOT live on Scratch** — moved to Synology SHR-2. Scratch = downloads/temp ONLY. Path mapping in `tool-guide.md`.\n- **macOS NFS mount gotchas**: `mapall` is NOT valid on macOS. UID 501≠1000 = write denied. Fix: Synology DSM squash rules PLUS chown to tyler:administrators (squash maps to admin UID 1026, not file owner UID 1000). Must remount after. diskutil unmount hangs on busy mounts. NEVER rm -rf mount points. Main=/volume1, VaultBoi1Main=/volume2 (different!).\n- **Synology SSH**: port 420 (non-standard), user tyler. Use `ssh -p 420 tyler@192.168.1.103` for permission fixes that can't be done via MCP (read-only account).\n- **DS1821+ has 4GB RAM min** — supports 32GB; DDR4 ECC SO-DIMMs ~$50-80 for 16GB upgrade\n- **Synology NFS admin = DSM admin account** — mcp-server needs admin for storage/disk/RAID/network APIs (error 105 without)\n- **SMB drive mappings drop across SSH** — Windows maps per-logon-session; use UNC paths in scripts\n- **PowerShell password escaping over SSH** — $, &, ! mangled through bash→ssh→cmd→PS chain; use net use or temp files\n- **Synology SSH admin paths** — sshpass on Mac, sudo -S for stdin password, synoshare at /usr/syno/sbin/, no crontab (edit /etc/crontab), DSM API 403 for share config (use SSH CLI), btrfs snapshots work direct\n- **WSL2 local vs NFS confusion** — /mnt/synology-nfs/server-manager/ on WSL2 is LOCAL storage not NFS-mounted; verify with `mount` command\n- **NFS `noauto` mount drops silently in WSL2** — fstab `noauto` means mounts drop between sessions; remount before each batch\n\n## Hardware / USB (`lessons/hardware.md`)\n- USB 3.0 radiates 2.4GHz RF — interferes with wireless dongles (G733) and USB audio (Yeti)\n- Blue Yeti draws 500mA — passive USB hubs can't power it reliably\n- Fix: dedicated USB 2.0 hub physically separated from USB 3.0 ports\n- Corsair keyboards expose 3-4 HID endpoints — slow KVM re-enumeration, worse with iCUE running\n- Mac Mini has no USB 2.0 ports — need USB 2.0 hub via adapter for audio isolation\n- HA voice satellites: ESP32-S3-BOX-3 supports on-device microWakeWord, Voice PE has echo cancellation\n- **GPU VRAM contention** — profile each service's VRAM; 1080 Ti can't run Chatterbox (6.2GB) + ComfyUI simultaneously; stop one first\n- **PSU wattage caused GPU Xid 79** — 400W insufficient for GTX 1060 120W TDP; upgraded to 850W Segotep GM850\n- **ASRock Z170M Extreme4 dual-GPU layout** — PCIE1+PCIE3 share 16 lanes (x8/x8); 1080 Ti + 1060, PCIE4 for future 10G NIC\n- **DDR4 prices skyrocketed in 2026** — cheapest 2x8GB ~$97 (was $25); AI-driven pricing on EOL DDR4\n- **ComfyUI VAEDecodeTiled for 8GB VRAM** — VAEDecode OOMs at 768x1024+; swap to VAEDecodeTiled (tile_size=512)\n- **WSL2 Store upgrade on Win10** — inbox WSL has no systemd; `wsl --update --web-download` to get Store version; requires reboot\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":106,"oldLines":6,"newStart":106,"newLines":7,"lines":[" - **PostToolUse hook for Discord relay** — checks discord-queue.md after tool calls; Midjourney: Official API / Web Access (Non-Discord)\n\n### Current state (March 2026)\n- **No public official API.** Enterprise API is invite-only application form at `updates.midjourney.com/enterprise-api-survey/` — no release date, no public access.\n- Web UI at midjourney.com IS the primary interface since 2024. Discord is now optional.\n- Web UI features: inpainting editor, folder organize, bulk download, draft mode, batch jobs (2K items), personalize, explore.\n- Web UI does NOT expose any programmatic interface or webhooks — it is SPA calling internal REST endpoints authenticated by session cookie.\n- `igolaizola/midjourney-automation` (Apify actor) extracts session cookie and calls those internal endpoints — NOT Playwright/DOM scraping. Still TOS-violating, direct ban risk (your cookie = your account).\n\n### Can a banned Discord user access MJ through midjourney.com?\n- **Almost certainly NO.** Account bans are platform-wide, not Discord-specific. The ban applies to the account (email/auth), not just Discord access.\n- When banned: lose access immediately, no refund, no exceptions — confirmed by multiple user reports.\n- Account deletion (voluntary or via ban) permanently deletes all images — MJ's own Data Deletion FAQ states account deletion removes all creations.\n- **There is NO documented case of a banned account retaining read-only web gallery access.** All user reports indicate full lockout.\n- Appeal path: contact support via midjourney.com or Discord. Generic denials are common; success rate is very low.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Midjourney: Image Export/Download After Ban\n\n### Bulk download (non-banned account)\n- Web UI Organize section has bulk download — confirmed feature as of 2025.\n- No API for automated download — requires manual action or browser automation (igolaizola actor can do bulk download as part of its pipeline, but this also violates TOS).\n\n### After ban\n- **Images are inaccessible.** The ban locks the account, which controls access to the gallery.\n- CDN URLs for individual images MAY remain accessible if you have the direct URL (e.g., `cdn.midjourney.com/...`) — CDN links from Discord attachments can persist separately from account status. But this is unreliable and undocumented.\n- **If you have Discord job IDs** (from when you generated in Discord), you can try `/show <job_id>` from another Discord account in a MJ bot channel — but this requires another active MJ subscription.\n- **Bottom line**: assume all images are lost when an account is banned. Mitigation: download everything with bulk export BEFORE any risky activity.\n\n### Preventive action\n- Use the Organize > bulk download regularly as a backup habit.\n- Discord attachment URLs are semi-permanent CDN links — can be downloaded separately via script.\n- Do NOT use annual billing if running automation — ban = no refund.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Kling AI Image Generation\n\n### What is it\n- Kuaishou (China) — primarily known for video AI, but now has dedicated image models:\n  - **Kling Image 01**: December 2025 — first dedicated image model\n  - **Kling Image 03 (3.0)**: February 4, 2026 — current flagship\n\n### Kling Image 03 technical capabilities\n- Visual Chain-of-Thought reasoning: reasons through scene composition before rendering (lighting logic, spatial relationships, mirror physics)\n- Diffusion Transformer + Deep-Stack architecture\n- Native 2K/4K generation (not upscaled — actual diffusion at high res)\n- Up to 10 reference images per generation\n- Image Series Mode: 2-9 sequential images with consistent style + narrative\n- Dual-reward reinforcement learning: balances photorealism and cinematic aesthetics\n- Understands cinematography language: \"low angle,\" \"85mm f/1.4,\" \"dutch tilt\" etc.\n- Intelligent aspect ratio detection from reference images\n\n### Kling Image quality vs Midjourney (anime/illustration)\n- **Kling Image 03 is photorealism-first, cinematic-first** — not optimized for anime/illustration flat art\n- **Midjourney (Niji 7)** produces more visually striking illustrative/stylized output for anime\n- For anime-style webtoon: MJ Niji 7 > Kling Image 03\n- For photorealistic manga (live-action cinematic style): Kling Image 03 competitive\n- For consistency in character-driven sequences: Kling Image Series Mode is interesting but benchmarks show subtle drift across 5+ panels\n- Multi-reference input (10 images) is a real advantage for character consistency — more capable than MJ which has no --cref in Niji 7\n- **Verdict**: Kling is NOT a direct MJ replacement for gothic anime/webtoon. Use it for: image-to-video generation from self-hosted SD outputs, or where cinematic composition language matters more than flat illustration style.\n\n### Kling API pricing (images)\n- Subscription plans: Standard $10/mo (660 credits, ~3,300 images at 0.2 credits each), Pro $37/mo (3,000 credits)\n- API credits: variable by output size and model version — 0.2 credits for standard image\n- At Pro tier (~$37/mo, 3,000 credits): ~15,000 standard images/mo = $0.0025/image\n- At Standard tier (~$10/mo, 660 credits): ~3,300 images/mo = $0.003/image\n- For 600 images: Standard plan covers it easily, ~$10/mo\n- Direct API available at `klingai.com/global/dev/`\n\n---\n\n## 4. Best Self-Hosted Models for Gothic Anime / Webtoon at Scale (600+ images)\n\n### Model rankings for gothic anime / dark webtoon style\n\n| Model | VRAM needed | Style fit | Speed (RTX 3090) | Commercial use |\n|-------|-------------|-----------|-------------------|----------------|\n| **Illustrious XL 2.0** | 8GB (medvram) / 12GB full | Excellent anime | ~200 img/hr @1024px | MIT-like, check Civitai terms |\n| **FLUX.1-dev** | 12-16GB (Q8/FP8) / 24GB (BF16) | Excellent, photorealistic-leaning | ~15-20s/img (RTX 3090) | Non-commercial (Apache for schnell) |\n| **FLUX.1-schnell** | 8GB (Q4) | Good, faster | 1-4 steps = 3-5s/img | Apache 2.0 — commercial OK |\n| **FLUX.2 [klein] 4B** | ~8GB | Good | Sub-second (Jan 2026) | Check BFL licensing |\n| **NovelAI Diffusion v3** | 8-12GB | Best for pure anime | Similar to Illustrious | Proprietary, no commercial |\n\n**Winner for gothic anime webtoon at scale: Illustrious XL 2.0 + LoRA stack in ComfyUI**\n\n### VRAM requirements by approach\n\n| GPU | VRAM | What runs |\n|-----|------|-----------|\n| GTX 1080 Ti | 11GB | Illustrious XL with `--medvram`; FLUX schnell Q4; FLUX Kontext FP4 GGUF (7GB). Use cu121 PyTorch — NOT cu128. |\n| RTX 3080 | 10-12GB | Same as above + FLUX dev FP8 borderline |\n| RTX 3090 | 24GB | Everything: Illustrious XL full, FLUX dev BF16, FLUX Kontext full |\n| RTX 4090 | 24GB | Same as 3090, ~2x faster |\n\n### Gothic anime LoRAs available on Civitai (Illustrious XL / SDXL)\n\n| LoRA | Platform | Style | Notes |\n|------|----------|-------|-------|\n| **Dark Anime Style** | Illustrious | Dark anime, high contrast | Updated Nov 2025, Illustrious-native |\n| **Gothic Detail Enhancer** | SDXL/Illustrious/Pony | Gothic atmosphere enhancer | Works across checkpoint families |\n| **Gothic Anime Style XL** | SDXL | Gothic anime character art | v1.0, older but works |\n| **Dark Gothic Fantasy** | SDXL | Gothic + fantasy themes, backgrounds | Good for environmental shots |\n| **Darkness/暗闇化 (Hotaru)** | Illustrious/Pony | Dark atmosphere technique | Characters bright on dark BG, dramatic |\n| **Goth/Emo Girl** | FLUX + SDXL | Character style | Available for both FLUX and SDXL |\n| **Anime High Contrast** | Illustrious | High-contrast manga look | Good for panel-style art |\n| **Manhwa Artstyle** | Illustrious/SDXL | Korean webtoon/manhwa | Civitai 257995, trigger: `manhwa-artstyle` |\n\n**Recommended stacking for gothic webtoon:**\n- Base: Illustrious XL 2.0\n- Style LoRA: `Manhwa Artstyle` (0.4-0.5) + `Dark Anime Style` (0.3-0.4)\n- Atmosphere LoRA: `Gothic Detail Enhancer` (0.3) OR `Darkness/暗闇化` (0.4)\n- Character LoRA: custom-trained per character (0.6-0.7)\n- Do NOT stack more than 3-4 LoRAs simultaneously — quality degrades\n\n### Generation time estimates at 20 steps, DPM++ 2M Karras\n\n| GPU | Resolution | Time/image | Images/hr |\n|-----|-----------|------------|-----------|\n| GTX 1080 Ti (medvram) | 1024x1024 | ~45-60s | ~70-80 |\n| RTX 3090 | 1024x1024 | ~15-18s | ~200-220 |\n| RTX 3090 | 1024x1536 (webtoon) | ~20-25s | ~160-180 |\n| RTX 4090 | 1024x1024 | ~7-10s | ~400-500 |\n\nFor 600 images on a single RTX 3090: ~3-4 hours batch run.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Flux.1 Pro: Self-Hosted vs API\n\n### Can Flux be self-hosted?\n\n**Yes. FLUX.1-dev and FLUX.1-schnell are open weights from Black Forest Labs.**\n- `black-forest-labs/FLUX.1-dev` — Apache 2.0 for non-commercial, commercial license available\n- `black-forest-labs/FLUX.1-schnell` — Apache 2.0 (commercial OK)\n- FLUX.2 models (klein, max) — newer, check per-model licensing\n\n### VRAM requirements (self-hosted Flux.1)\n\n| Quantization | VRAM | Notes |\n|-------------|------|-------|\n| BF16 (full) | 24GB | RTX 3090/4090 only |\n| FP8 | ~12-13GB | RTX 3080 Ti, 3090 at lower VRAM |\n| Q8 | ~13GB | Similar to FP8 |\n| Q6-Q4 | 8-11GB | RTX 3080, 4070. Noticeable quality drop at Q4 |\n| FP4 GGUF (Kontext) | 7GB | Quantized Kontext — 97.2% vs BF16 quality |\n\n- RTX 3090 (24GB): runs FLUX.1-dev at full BF16, ~12-15s/image\n- GTX 1080 Ti (11GB): runs Q6/Q4 quantized only — not recommended for FLUX full model. Illustrious XL is better use of this GPU.\n\n### FLUX.1-schnell vs dev\n\n- **Schnell**: 1-4 steps, ~3-5s/img on RTX 3090, Apache 2.0 (commercial OK), quality slightly lower\n- **Dev**: 20-30 steps, ~12-20s/img on RTX 3090, non-commercial weights, higher quality\n- Quality gap: dev wins on coherence and detail; schnell is usable and fast\n\n### FLUX.2 [klein] — newest (January 2026)\n- 4B parameter model, fits ~8GB VRAM\n- Sub-second generation on consumer GPUs (RTX 3090/4070+)\n- Still checking licensing status\n\n### API pricing comparison\n\n| Provider | Model | Price/image (1024x1024) |\n|----------|-------|------------------------|\n| **bfl.ai (Black Forest Labs)** | FLUX.2 Pro | $0.03/megapixel → $0.03 at 1MP |\n| **fal.ai** | FLUX.2 Pro | $0.03/image (1MP) |\n| **fal.ai** | FLUX.1-dev | ~$0.025-0.035/image |\n| **Replicate** | FLUX.1-dev | ~$0.055/image (GPU time based) |\n| **fal.ai** | FLUX.1-schnell | ~$0.003-0.005/image |\n\n### Self-hosted vs API cost break-even\n\nFor 600 images at $0.03/image (fal.ai FLUX.2 Pro): $18 total.\n\nSelf-hosted electricity + hardware amortization:\n- RTX 3090 running FLUX.1-dev: 600 images × 15s = 2.5hr GPU time\n- At 350W TDP: 2.5hr × 0.35kW = 0.875 kWh ≈ $0.10-0.15 electricity\n- If you own the GPU: self-hosting wins by far at scale.\n- If you need to rent cloud GPU: RunPod RTX 3090 at ~$0.44/hr × 2.5hr = ~$1.10 vs $18 API — self-host on cloud wins too.\n\n**Verdict: For 600+ images on owned hardware, self-host always wins on cost. API wins for convenience/no-setup.**\n\n---\n\n## Recommendation Summary\n\n**For gothic anime webtoon at 600+ images:**\n1. **Primary**: Self-hosted Illustrious XL 2.0 in ComfyUI on RTX 3090 (or RTX 3080 Ti). Layer `Manhwa Artstyle` + `Dark Anime Style` + `Gothic Detail Enhancer` LoRAs. Use ControlNet (AnimeLineArt + ZoeDepth) for structural consistency. Train per-character LoRAs. ~200 img/hr.\n2. **Fallback API**: fal.ai FLUX.1-schnell (~$0.003-0.005/img) for rapid iteration without GPU overhead.\n3. **Avoid**: Kling Image for anime/illustration style — it's photorealism-first. Use Kling for image-to-video conversion if needed.\n4. **MJ ban recovery**: If banned, assume images are gone. The ban is account-wide, not Discord-specific. Act before the ban: bulk-export from Organize tab regularly. CDN URLs from Discord DMs may survive short-term.\n\n---\n\n## Sources\n- https://apatero.com/blog/midjourney-banned-account-alternatives-2025 (MJ ban behavior, alternatives)\n- https://www.mindstudio.ai/blog/what-is-kling-image-03 (Kling Image 03 technical spec)\n- https://www.mindstudio.ai/blog/what-is-kling-image-01 (Kling image gen history)\n- https://klingai.com/global/dev/pricing (Kling API pricing)\n- https://aitoolanalysis.com/kling-ai-pricing/ (Kling pricing breakdown, credit costs)\n- https://pxz.ai/blog/flux-dev-vs-schnell (Flux dev vs schnell comparison)\n- https://www.aifreeapi.com/en/posts/flux-kontext-local-deployment (FLUX Kontext VRAM table, FP4 quality retention)\n- https://insiderllm.com/guides/flux-locally-complete-guide/ (FLUX self-hosting guide)\n- https://www.hardware-corner.net/guides/gpu-for-flux-1-image-model/ (GPU VRAM requirements)\n- https://fal.ai/pricing (fal.ai FLUX pricing)\n- https://bfl.ai/pricing (BFL official pricing)\n- https://pricepertoken.com/image (API pricing comparison table)\n- https://civitai.com/models/2134339/dark-anime-style (Dark Anime Style LoRA, Illustrious)\n- https://civitai.com/models/1478407/gothic-detail-enhancer-sdxl-illustrious-pony (Gothic Detail Enhancer LoRA)\n- https://civitai.com/models/341440/gothic-anime-style (Gothic Anime Style XL LoRA)\n- https://civitai.com/models/902671/sdxl-darkness (Darkness/暗闇化 LoRA)\n- https://civitai.com/models/458635/goth-emo-girl-lora-flux-sdxl (Goth/Emo Girl LoRA, FLUX+SDXL)\n- https://github.com/black-forest-labs/flux2 (FLUX.2 official repo)\n- /Users/eviemini/.claude/projects/-Users-eviemini-Documents-code-server-manager/02610c2a-bd14-4804-96cd-a256bf0aa29c.jsonl:{"parentUuid":"6b44a354-fc13-4360-9cad-69cfb7cbc01e","isSidechain":false,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"02610c2a-bd14-4804-96cd-a256bf0aa29c","version":"2.1.71","gitBranch":"HEAD","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_019eH8ENZqGp5DBiw1oVmkLg","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→# Lessons Index\n     2→\n     3→One-liner per lesson. Read this first, then fetch the relevant topic file.\n     4→\n     5→## MCP (`lessons/mcp.md`)\n     6→- stdout pollution breaks MCP stdio transport — use console.error() only\n     7→- Discord MCP token must be prefixed with \"Bot \" manually\n     8→- Use fetch MCP for HTTP calls, not raw curl\n     9→- **lgtv MCP**: `node --import tsx` fails in Claude Code — use `node_modules/.bin/tsx` binary directly\n    10→- **grafana MCP**: env var is `GRAFANA_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_TOKEN`, not `GRAFANA_TOKEN`\n    11→- **NFS-backed fleet reminders** — JSONL on NFS, shell scripts for create/resolve/check, integrated into session-start + watchdog hooks\n    12→\n    13→## Home Assistant (`lessons/ha.md`)\n    14→- Cross-subnet discovery fails — HA can't find IoT devices without second NIC\n    15→- HAOS won't boot with UEFI Secure Boot enabled (pre-enrolled-keys=1)\n    16→- Proxmox API import-from silently fails on xz-compressed qcow2\n    17→- HA REST API: use Bearer token with curl (fetch MCP lacks auth headers)\n    18→- Integration pairing: all headless via config flow API except Plex (needs OAuth)\n    19→- Cross-subnet fix: add net1 on vmbr1 to HA VM, no VLAN tag needed\n    20→- Ollama subentries: use config subentries API for conversation agents\n    21→- **Wyoming config flow**: REST API only — `POST /api/config/config_entries/flow` then submit host/port\n    22→- **Config entry delete**: `DELETE /api/config/config_entries/entry/<id>` (WebSocket `config_entries/delete` = unknown_command)\n    23→\n    24→## Docker (`lessons/docker.md`)\n    25→- **CRITICAL: NEVER deploy fresh containers over existing Plex config — back up Preferences.xml first, use tools/media-deploy.sh**\n    26→- **CRITICAL: NEVER run `docker compose down -v` on media stack — `-v` deletes bind-mounted config data**\n    27→- Plex MachineIdentifier is irreversible (SHA1 hash) — loss = all shared users disconnected permanently\n    28→- Homepage config: write via Synology MCP, can't verify via fetch (Next.js SPA)\n    29→- Portainer admin timeout: recreate container if init window missed\n    30→- Uptime Kuma uses socket.io, not REST — must use web UI for initial setup\n    31→- Container Manager port conflicts: DSM reserves 80/443, use alternates\n    32→- **GPU node (192.168.1.67) ≠ media-server VM 200 (192.168.1.200)** — GPU workloads go to bare-metal GPU node, media stack is on VM 200 with NO GPU\n    33→- Docker on ZFS: requires \"storage-driver\": \"zfs\" in daemon.json\n    34→- WSL2 Docker IPv6 binding: ports bind to [::1] only, use v4tov6 portproxy\n    35→- WSL2 + no-systemd: use /etc/wsl.conf [boot] command= to auto-start services\n    36→- WSL2 from SSH: hangs in session 0 — use Windows Scheduled Task with /ru INTERACTIVE\n    37→- WSL2 LxssManager stuck STOP_PENDING: use WMI Terminate (taskkill/Stop-Process fail even as admin)\n    38→- **SLOW LESSON**: WSL over SSH is a trap — always use the Scheduled Task playbook from the start, never debug iteratively. See docker.md for full playbook.\n    39→- **MusicGen/audiocraft Docker**: PyAV can't compile from source — use `--only-binary=av`. Never conda in pytorch images (upgrades torch). Use `--no-deps audiocraft` + manual deps. Deployed `illustrator-poll.js` on VM 150.\n   123→- **CRITICAL — Kling URL capture grabs wrong videos** — `_extract_urls()` grabbed ANY .mp4 URL from ANY API response, including other users' videos in the feed gallery. Returns \"Invalid parameter\". Scratch = downloads/temp ONLY. Read this first, then fetch the relevant topic file.\n\n## MCP (`lessons/mcp.md`)\n- stdout pollution breaks MCP stdio transport — use console.error() only\n- Discord MCP token must be prefixed with \"Bot \" manually\n- Use fetch MCP for HTTP calls, not raw curl\n- **lgtv MCP**: `node --import tsx` fails in Claude Code — use `node_modules/.bin/tsx` binary directly\n- **grafana MCP**: env var is `GRAFANA_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_TOKEN`, not `GRAFANA_TOKEN`\n- **NFS-backed fleet reminders** — JSONL on NFS, shell scripts for create/resolve/check, integrated into session-start + watchdog hooks\n\n## Home Assistant (`lessons/ha.md`)\n- Cross-subnet discovery fails — HA can't find IoT devices without second NIC\n- HAOS won't boot with UEFI Secure Boot enabled (pre-enrolled-keys=1)\n- Proxmox API import-from silently fails on xz-compressed qcow2\n- HA REST API: use Bearer token with curl (fetch MCP lacks auth headers)\n- Integration pairing: all headless via config flow API except Plex (needs OAuth)\n- Cross-subnet fix: add net1 on vmbr1 to HA VM, no VLAN tag needed\n- Ollama subentries: use config subentries API for conversation agents\n- **Wyoming config flow**: REST API only — `POST /api/config/config_entries/flow` then submit host/port\n- **Config entry delete**: `DELETE /api/config/config_entries/entry/<id>` (WebSocket `config_entries/delete` = unknown_command)\n\n## Docker (`lessons/docker.md`)\n- **CRITICAL: NEVER deploy fresh containers over existing Plex config — back up Preferences.xml first, use tools/media-deploy.sh**\n- **CRITICAL: NEVER run `docker compose down -v` on media stack — `-v` deletes bind-mounted config data**\n- Plex MachineIdentifier is irreversible (SHA1 hash) — loss = all shared users disconnected permanently\n- Homepage config: write via Synology MCP, can't verify via fetch (Next.js SPA)\n- Portainer admin timeout: recreate container if init window missed\n- Uptime Kuma uses socket.io, not REST — must use web UI for initial setup\n- Container Manager port conflicts: DSM reserves 80/443, use alternates\n- **GPU node (192.168.1.67) ≠ media-server VM 200 (192.168.1.200)** — GPU workloads go to bare-metal GPU node, media stack is on VM 200 with NO GPU\n- Docker on ZFS: requires \"storage-driver\": \"zfs\" in daemon.json\n- WSL2 Docker IPv6 binding: ports bind to [::1] only, use v4tov6 portproxy\n- WSL2 + no-systemd: use /etc/wsl.conf [boot] command= to auto-start services\n- WSL2 from SSH: hangs in session 0 — use Windows Scheduled Task with /ru INTERACTIVE\n- WSL2 LxssManager stuck STOP_PENDING: use WMI Terminate (taskkill/Stop-Process fail even as admin)\n- **SLOW LESSON**: WSL over SSH is a trap — always use the Scheduled Task playbook from the start, never debug iteratively. Also: stale results file fallback in mj-hopper.sh read old batch-results — must compare against pre-existing file.\n- **MJ --no + --niji 7 conflict** — `--no` parameter is incompatible with `--niji 7` mode. Returns \"Invalid parameter\". Strip `--no` segments from niji prompts.\n\n## Gemini API / Model Routing (`lessons/media.md`)\n- **Gemini as coding agent**: keep tasks tiny (1 file change), send complete files not diffs, watch for truncation on 500+ line files\n- **gemini-code.sh pattern**: generate→apply→build→fix loop, auto-restores backups on failure\n- **Model availability**: 2.5 Flash works reliably; 2.0 Flash Lite quota takes hours to provision on new projects; 3.1 Flash Lite gets overloaded easily\n- **Import mutation**: Gemini sometimes changes unrelated imports (e.g. @dnd-kit/sortable → @dnd-kit/core) — always diff-review before committing\n\n## Storage / NAS (`lessons/storage.md`)\n- Synology MCP = FileStation only — query DSM API directly for storage health (SYNO.Storage.CGI.Storage)\n- Synology \"attention\" status = capacity warning (fs_almost_full), not disk failure\n- SHR pools spanning expansion units = eSATA SPOF — isolate each DX517 as its own pool\n- TrueNAS VM 104 uses physical passthrough via /dev/disk/by-id/ paths, not virtual disks\n- PVE hardware discovery: /nodes/pve/disks/list + /nodes/pve/hardware/pci via API token auth\n- TrueNAS Scratch degradation: ZFS metadata corruption from unclean shutdowns, not disk failure\n- **Persistent data must NOT live on Scratch** — moved to Synology SHR-2. Scratch = downloads/temp ONLY. Path mapping in `tool-guide.md`.\n\n## Hardware / USB (`lessons/hardware.md`)\n- USB 3.0 radiates 2.4GHz RF — interferes with wireless dongles (G733) and USB audio (Yeti)\n- Blue Yeti draws 500mA — passive USB hubs can't power it reliably\n- Fix: dedicated USB 2.0 hub physically separated from USB 3.0 ports\n- Corsair keyboards expose 3-4 HID endpoints — slow KVM re-enumeration, worse with iCUE running\n- Mac Mini has no USB 2.0 ports — need USB 2.0 hub via adapter for audio isolation\n- HA voice satellites: ESP32-S3-BOX-3 supports on-device microWakeWord, Voice PE has echo cancellation\n","numLines":148,"startLine":1,"totalLines":148}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"6b44a354-fc13-4360-9cad-69cfb7cbc01e"}
